Two independent API-freeze cleanups (spec §17 B30), both scoped by a 3-agent understand-phase sweep before implementation. **Part 1 — postfx `createColorGradingLut` param order.** Spec asked to flip `(uniforms, id?)` → `(id, uniforms)` to match the 11 sibling `create*` factories. **Blocker the spec missed** (surfaced by the sweep): the current `(uniforms, id?)` order was a DELIBERATE, documented choice — [SESSION_LOG.md:3721](SESSION_LOG.md), session #122 — forced by Biome `useDefaultParameterLast`: because `uniforms.lut` is required, `uniforms` cannot default to `{}`, so a defaulted `id` placed first re-triggers the rule. You cannot have {id-first + id-defaulted + uniforms-required} together. **Owner picked Option A at the approval stop: `id` required, no default** — `createColorGradingLut(id: string, uniforms: { lut; intensity? })`. Achieves the positional `(id, uniforms)` parity the row wants with zero lint suppression; the lost id-default is immaterial because `uniforms` is mandatory so every caller passes an id regardless. (Options B [biome-ignore for full parity] and C [wontfix, respect prior decision] were declined.) **Blast radius verified minimal:** the ONLY callers in the entire repo are the 6 in `color-grading-lut.test.ts` (no production/app/render-web caller; not re-exported by the `@flare-engine/engine` umbrella — postfx isn't a barrel dep). All 6 updated (`(uniforms)`/`(uniforms,'lut')` → `('color-grading-lut'|'lut', uniforms)`); the "default factory" test renamed (no default exists now). Only doc referencing the signature is `api-specs.md:465` — updated to `(id, uniforms)` + the Biome rationale. Shipped `.d.ts` verified via forced fresh build: `createColorGradingLut(id: string, uniforms: {...})`. **Part 2 — create-flare-app `TEMPLATES satisfies Record<…>`.** `manifest.ts:16` `Record<string, TemplateManifest>` annotation → `= {…} satisfies Record<string, TemplateManifest>`, so `keyof typeof TEMPLATES` collapses from `string|number` to the literal union `'blank'|'topdown'|'shmup'`; `TemplateId` (:41) and `isTemplateId` (:43) auto-narrow, untouched. Sweep verdict LOW risk — every `TEMPLATES` usage is a read (Object.keys/hasOwn/keyed reads), no arbitrary-key writes, no plain-string→TemplateId assignment; scaffold.ts/cli.ts stay type-clean (the now-redundant `if (!manifest)` guard + `manifest?.` chains still compile). **Scope correction:** the spec's "isTemplateId becomes a real guard both CLIs reuse (B13/HYG8)" is aspirational — neither `packages/cli` nor create-flare-app's own `cli.ts` imports `isTemplateId` today (each hardcodes a duplicate `parseTemplateArg`); that dedupe is separate HYG-class work, **left out of scope**. **Done-when 'type-level test':** existing manifest tests were runtime-only. Added two tests to `scaffold.test.ts` — a pure compile-time `Equal<TemplateId, 'blank'|'topdown'|'shmup'>` assertion, and a runtime narrowing test carrying an embedded compile-time `string→TemplateId` narrowing check. **Enforced by ROOT tsc, not the per-package gate** ([[typecheck_gate_skips_tests]]: per-package tsc is src-only; `bun test` strips types). **Proven discriminating:** reverting the `satisfies` fails root tsc at both assertions (scaffold.test.ts:40 `Type 'true' is not assignable to 'false'` + :51 `Type 'string' is not assignable to '"blank"|"topdown"|"shmup"'`); restored → clean. **Bookkeeping:** postfx **minor** changeset (public signature change; behavior-preserving; fixed-lockstep → 0.3.0, never major — [[oss_changeset_bump_lockstep]]); create-flare-app is `private` → no changeset (`check:changeset` skips private + src-only; confirmed green, 30 pkgs covered). Pre-gate friction caught ([[lint_gate_skips_scripts]]): biome would have failed the `Equal` type line's formatting in the test (gate lints src/ only) — reformatted before the gate; husky would otherwise have unstaged it. - **OSS-B29 — render: SpriteBatch bucket pooling — SUPERSEDED by B24; guard test added** (2026-07-08, session #233). Twenty-eighth Arc-OSS row (engine; P1, hot-path). `packages/render/tests/sprite-batch.test.ts` + `docs/plans/road-to-oss-2026-09.md` (§17 B29 spec corrected). **Finding:** the row/§17 premise (`flush()` re-allocating a `{image,effect,alpha,commands:[]}` bucket per unique key, discarded via `batches.length=0` at :221, with a dead `commands.length=0` loop at :218-220) describes the PRE-B24 design. Commit `e271eb2` (OSS-B24, same day, session #228) already rewrote `sprite-batch.ts` from the multi-bucket-per-key allocator to a **single reused streaming run-bucket** (`_batches[0]`): allocated once (lazily, :187-191), reused every frame, its `commands` array reset in place via `.length=0` (identity preserved, never re-`[]`). There is **no** `batches.length=0` in the current file, and the two `commands.length=0` calls (:208 mid-loop run reset, :224 final/frame reset) are **required, not dead** — dropping the :224 clear makes the next frame's first command compare against a stale `run.image` with `commands.length>0` and emit a spurious drawAtlas of the previous frame's now-overwritten pooled commands. The row's done-when (zero bucket allocation in an effect/alpha-heavy frame after warm-up) was **already satisfied**. **Chose (owner approval-stop, recommended option): guard test + fix spec — NOT the spec's multi-bucket-pool-with-live-count**, which would *regress* memory + complexity vs the streaming design for zero benefit. **Delivered:** an instrumented zero-alloc guard in `sprite-batch.test.ts` ("bucket pooling (zero-alloc)", +2 tests): an 8-sprite frame where no two consecutive share `(image,effect,alpha)` fans into 8 distinct runs (sanity-asserted) yet uses exactly ONE bucket; flushing it 100× keeps `_batches.length===1` with stable bucket-object AND inner-`commands`-array identity (`.toBe`) — proof no bucket or array is re-allocated per frame. Privates read via a typed `as unknown as {...}` cast (no `any`). Discriminating by construction: pre-B24 code grew `_batches` to one entry per run and discarded it each flush, so `_batches.length===1` + identity-stable both fail against it. §17 B29 rewritten to record the supersession + the delivered guard (one-source-per-fact); the todo Active pointer removed. **No `src` change** → `check:changeset` is a no-op (only `src/**` counts) so **no changeset / no lockstep bump**; no user-facing strings (no localization cascade); no render/react export added/renamed (no `game-view`/`safe-area` mock-stub rule). Gate green (lint/typecheck/test/build FULL TURBO/deps/changeset). SESSION_LOG #233 canonical.
- **OSS-B28 — ui + ui-shop: focus side-effect + shop refetch** (2026-07-08, session #232). Twenty-seventh Arc-OSS row (engine; P1; coord B15/M1). `packages/ui/src/{focus.ts,focus-provider.tsx}` + `packages/ui/tests/{focus.test.ts,primitives.test.ts}` + `packages/ui-shop/src/ShopScreen.tsx` + `packages/ui-shop/tests/ShopScreen.test.ts` + `.changeset/oss-b28-ui-shop-focus-refetch.md`. **Path correction:** spec cited `ShopPanel.tsx:39-53`; no such file — B20 renamed ShopPanel→`ShopScreen.tsx` (the `fetched`-ref/effect lines match exactly). (`packages/ui/src/shop-panel.tsx` is a *different* presentational primitive with no fetch — untouched.) **Part 1 — ui focus side-effect (focus.ts:92-95 accurate):** `createFocusManager().register()` auto-focused the first enabled registrant; since `Button` registers in a mount effect and paints a `borderWidth:2` ring when `focusedId===id`, the first enabled Button showed a **permanent focus ring with no input** on a touch tree, and it focused the *just-registered* node (mount order), contradicting the tabIndex contract at `focus.ts:12`. **Fix:** `register()` is now side-effect-free — `focusedId` stays `null` until a driver acts. Explicit opt-in added: `FocusManager.focusFirst()` (selects `orderedIds()[0]`, tab-order first, disabled-filtered) + `FocusProvider` `autoFocus?: boolean` prop (a `useEffect([autoFocus, mgr])` calls `focusFirst()` iff nothing is focused; relies on React child-before-parent effect order so all Buttons are registered first). Both additive (`FocusManager` +method, `FocusProviderProps` +optional prop). **Part 2 — ui-shop refetch (ShopScreen.tsx:39-53):** the once-for-life `fetched` ref made the fetch run once for the component's life while deps were `[catalog, provider, onError]`, so a Noop→concrete provider swap never refetched. **Fix (spec option 1):** effect keyed `[catalog, provider]`, resets `products`/`loadError` to null on change (back to loading), `cancelled` cleanup flag drops a stale previous-provider response; `onError` dropped from deps and read via a latest-ref (`onErrorRef.current = onError` in render) so a fresh inline callback identity can't storm-refetch. **Tests:** `focus.test.ts` rewritten (~7 tests baked in the removed auto-focus — each now focuses explicitly; the `auto-focuses the first registered node` test flipped to `register() does not focus`; +`focusFirst` tab-order/disabled/empty-manager tests); `primitives.test.ts` +3 (`without autoFocus → null`, `autoFocus focuses tab-order-first`, and fixed the `Disabled Button ignores press` test which relied on auto-focus); `ShopScreen.test.ts` +2 (`swapping the provider refetches + resets to loading` = done-when; `fetch failure surfaced through the latest onError ref`). ui 38 pass (was 35), ui-shop 15 pass (was 13). **Discrimination proven:** the refetch test **fails on the pre-fix src** (5 pass/1 fail via `git stash`) — but only after upgrading the ui-shop harness's `useRef` mock from a fresh `{current}`-per-render to a **persistent `refStore`** (the non-persistent mock reset the old `fetched` guard to false each render, masking the persisting-ref bug → the test was theater without it). **Friction recorded** (SESSION_LOG #232): hand-rolled React hook mocks must persist refs across simulated re-renders or multi-render tests are non-discriminating; related [[packages_react_test_mock_pollution]]. **Adversarial verification** (3-lens Workflow — focus-semantics · refetch-race · test-rigor, all `effort:high`): all **SOUND**, zero blockers. Concerns/nits folded in: (a) `autoFocus` is mount-time only (won't catch async/late-mounting children) → documented in the prop JSDoc, pointing dynamic sets at a driver; (b) refetch now keys on catalog/provider identity so an *unstable* reference storms → documented the stable-reference contract on `ShopScreenProps.catalog` (inherent to the spec's chosen option 1, idiomatic singleton/memoized usage unaffected); (c) the empty-manager `focusFirst` test overstated "clears" (focus was already null) → renamed to the honest "no-op, stays null not undefined", still discriminating the `?? null` guard. Left as known harness limits (noted, not fixable without a real renderer): the child-before-parent effect-order rationale and the onError-identity-doesn't-refetch decoupling can't be exercised by the manual hook mock. **No new user-facing strings (no localization cascade); ui/ui-shop are not render/react (no react mock-stub rule).** Changeset: ui + ui-shop **minor** (additive API + behavior change) — fixed-lockstep bumps all 40 to 0.3.0, never major ([[oss_changeset_bump_lockstep]] avoided; `check:changeset` OK, 30 pkgs covered). Gate green (lint/typecheck/test/build FULL TURBO/deps/changeset) + root tsc clean on tests; post-verification doc/test tweaks re-checked green (ui 38/ui-shop 15/biome/tsc). - **OSS-B27 — iap-revenuecat: configure-once race** (2026-07-08, session #231). Twenty-sixth Arc-OSS row (engine; P1). `packages/iap-revenuecat/src/provider.ts` + `packages/iap-revenuecat/tests/provider.test.ts` + `.changeset/oss-b27-iap-revenuecat-configure-once-race.md`. **Bug (spec §17 B27):** the row/spec cited `provider.ts:82-92`, but that region is `getSDK` (correct); the real race is in **`ensureConfigured` (:94-104)** — the `configured` boolean flipped only AFTER two awaits (`getSDK()` then `import('react-native')`), so concurrent first callers via `Promise.all([getProducts(), restore()])` all passed the `!configured` guard and each called `Purchases.configure`, which RevenueCat rejects as a double-configure (real line named in the plan per the stale-spec-path rule). **Fix:** replace the boolean with a memoized `configurePromise` awaited by every entrypoint (`getProducts`/`purchase`/`restore` all funnel through `ensureConfigured`), so `configure` runs exactly once for all in-flight callers. A failed configure clears the memo so a later call retries, preserving the old flag's stay-unset-on-throw semantics — with a **compare-and-clear guard** (`if (configurePromise === pending)`) so a stale second-awaiter's `catch` can't null a fresh promise a later caller already installed. Zero API-surface change (no `.d.ts`/barrel/api-specs churn; no user-facing strings → no localization cascade; not a render/react export → no react mock-stub rule). **Tests** (+3, extended the existing file — mock-pollution-safe): "concurrent first getProducts+restore call configure exactly once" (**discriminating** — reverted the src via `git stash` and confirmed it fails 2≠1 on the old code, passes on the fix); "a failed configure is retried on the next call, not permanently poisoned" (guards the new catch-reset branch — without the reset the 2nd call returns the cached rejection and configure never re-runs); "concurrent first callers share one rejected configure, then retry succeeds" (the untested intersection surfaced by review — both share one rejected promise, no unhandled rejection, each surfaces `[]` via its own `onError`, configure ran once for the pair, later call retries). 17 iap-revenuecat tests pass (was 14). **Adversarial verification** (3-lens Workflow — microtask/interleaving correctness · behavioral parity vs the old boolean · test rigor, all `effort:high`): all **SOUND**, zero blockers. Two `nit`s, **both folded in this row**: (a) the parity lens flagged the unconditional `catch` reset as a theoretical triple-configure under a precise interleaving during a *failed* configure → fixed by the compare-and-clear guard above (not a regression vs the old boolean, but now closed outright); (b) the test lens named the concurrent-reject intersection as the highest-value missing test → added as the 3rd test. **Friction:** verify.sh biome lints `src/` only, not `tests/` — the new test's long `Promise.all` line was format-clean to the gate but would have been unstaged by husky lint-staged at commit; caught by a pre-gate `biome check` on the changed test and wrapped multi-line (same class as the B26 friction; a `tests/`-inclusive gate lint would close it). Changeset: iap-revenuecat **minor** (matches B12) — fixed-lockstep bumps all 40 to 0.3.0, never major ([[oss_changeset_bump_lockstep]] avoided; `check:changeset` OK, 30 pkgs covered). Gate green (lint/typecheck/test/build FULL TURBO/deps/changeset); post-gate hardening re-verified via package test 17/build CJS+ESM+dual-.d.ts/biome/tsc, all clean. SESSION_LOG #231 canonical.
- **OSS-B26 — shapes: ShipSpec bounds include weapons + engine** (2026-07-08, session #230). Twenty-fifth Arc-OSS row (engine; P1). `packages/shapes/src/bounds.ts` + `packages/shapes/tests/bounds.test.ts` + `.changeset/oss-b26-shapes-ship-bounds-weapons-engine.md`. **Bug (spec §17 B26):** `ShipSpec` = `hull` + `weapons: RectSpec[]` + `engine: SquareSpec` (painted engine→hull→weapons by `drawShip`; the engine square is rendered as a `w=h=size` rect by `drawSquare`, and `SquareSpec` is deliberately NOT a member of the flat `ShapeSpec` union), but both bounds functions used the hull ALONE — `singleShapeRadius('ship')`→`singleShapeRadius(spec.hull)` (:39-40, feeds the per-frame glow-ring radius at `draw.ts:108-109`) and `boundsR('ship')`→`shapeBoundsRadius([spec.hull])` (:69-70, sizes the atlas tile). So a wing weapon mounted at an `ox`/`oy` beyond the hull radius, or the offset engine square, was rasterized into a hull-sized atlas tile and clipped by the glow ring. **Fix:** both now return `max(reach)` across the hull, every weapon (`hypot(ox,oy)+radius`), and the engine. `singleShapeRadius('ship')` is on the per-frame glow path → **zero-alloc** indexed loop mirroring `drawShip`'s style (undefined-guard for `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`), engine via `hypot(engine.ox,engine.oy)+engine.size*0.6` (the rect face factor). `boundsR('ship')` is atlas-build-only → reuses `shapeBoundsRadius([hull, ...weapons, {engine as w=h=size rect}])`, preserving the existing `+2`/`max(4)` margin machinery unchanged. **Tests:** the two existing ship tests keep their exact values (33 glow / 29 tile — hull dominates both fixtures, verified by hand) but were renamed off the now-misleading "delegates to its hull" titles; 4 new fixtures pin hand-computed numbers — wing-weapon glow `hypot(30,5)+14*0.6=38.8138`, offset-engine glow `40+10*0.6=46`, wing-weapon tile `hypot(30,5)+14*0.75+2, +2 outer=44.9138`, each with a strictly-greater-than-hull regression guard. 51 shapes tests pass (was 47). `README.md` left untouched — its bounds examples make no hull-only ship claim (grep-verified). **Adversarial verification** (3-lens Workflow — math recomputation · edge/type/alloc · spec-fidelity/blast-radius): all **PASS**, zero confirmed defects; independent from-scratch recomputation of all 6 assertions agreed; only `confirmed:false` nits (latent `exactOptionalPropertyTypes` fragility [not enabled in tsconfig] + the pre-existing rect-glow `0.6` corner-under-cover, both out of B26 scope — the tile `0.75` factor always contains a rect corner, and the glow ring's `+4`/`+2` margin covers it). **Friction:** the builder's self-check ran typecheck+test but not lint — biome's line-width rule would have failed the gate on the inline engine-rect literal; caught by a pre-gate biome check and wrapped multi-line before the gate ran. No exported symbol added/renamed (no react mock-stub rule); no user-facing strings (no localization cascade). Changeset: shapes **minor** — fixed-lockstep bumps all 40 to 0.3.0, never major ([[oss_changeset_bump_lockstep]] avoided; `check:changeset` OK, 30 pkgs covered). Gate green (lint/typecheck/test/build FULL TURBO/deps/changeset). SESSION_LOG #230 canonical.
- **OSS-B25 — share + deep-links: platform-adapter correctness** (2026-07-08, session #229). Twenty-fourth Arc-OSS row (engine; P1). `packages/share/src/share.ts` + `packages/share/tests/share.test.ts` + `packages/deep-links/src/sources.ts` + `packages/deep-links/tests/manager.test.ts` + `.changeset/oss-b25-share-deeplinks-adapter-correctness.md`. Two platform-adapter correctness bugs (spec §17 B25). **(1) share — iOS Cancel misreported dispatched:true.** `createNativeShareTransport` computed `dispatched: res.action !== 'dismissed'`, but RN's `Share.share` resolves to `Share.sharedAction` (`'sharedAction'`) or `Share.dismissedAction` (`'dismissedAction'`) — **never** a bare `'dismissed'` — so an iOS cancel (`'dismissedAction'`) satisfied `!== 'dismissed'` and reported `dispatched:true`. Fixed to `res.action === 'sharedAction'` (share.ts:129). The existing test at `share.test.ts:89` faked `{action:'dismissed'}` (a value RN never returns — it agreed with the bug and so never caught it); rewritten to return the real `'dismissedAction'` constant, which fails on the old code and passes on the fix. Android always resolves `'sharedAction'` once the chooser launches (no cancel signal) → unchanged `dispatched:true`, matching the "kicked off successfully" contract. **(2) deep-links — shared popstate listener killed navigation for all.** `createWebSource` added one shared `onPop` closure per `subscribe()` call; the real DOM dedupes identical `(type, listener, capture)` registrations, so the FIRST subscriber's `removeEventListener` removed the only registration and stopped `popstate` navigation for every remaining subscriber. Fixed by **ref-counting the DOM listener via true Set cardinality** — `addEventListener` only when `listeners.size === 1` (first add), `removeEventListener` only when `listeners.size === 0` (last remove). Using `.size` (not a boolean flag) makes it robust to same-handler-twice (Set dedup), stale/double unsubscribe, and unsubscribe-during-dispatch. **(3) live-Set dispatch (2026-07-08 audit add, folded from B23's scope-check).** Both `onPop` (:48) and `notify` (:63) iterated the live `listeners` Set, so a handler that subscribes mid-dispatch was visited in that same dispatch; both now snapshot with `for (const l of [...listeners])` — a deliberate, documented "(un)subscribe during dispatch must not change who receives this dispatch" policy, symmetric with the tested subscribe-during-dispatch case. **Tests** (extended existing files, no new files — mock-pollution-safe): `share.test.ts` "reports undispatched on the real RN dismissedAction (iOS cancel)"; `manager.test.ts` gains a **DOM-faithful (deduping) window mock** helper (the existing `createWebSource` mock intentionally allows duplicate registrations, so it can't exercise dedup) + "one unsubscribe keeps popstate live for the remaining subscriber" (fails on the shared-listener bug) + "a subscribe during popstate dispatch does not receive that dispatch". Both packages ran fresh (cache miss): deep-links 30 pass / share 25 pass, 0 fail. **Out of scope, flagged not touched:** `sources.ts:23` `createMemorySource.push` shares the live-Set pattern but the B25 spec scopes the snapshot fix to `createWebSource` (:48/:63) only; the deep-links `parse.ts`/`router.ts` prototype read-leak is **B23b**, not this row. No exported symbol added/renamed (no react mock-stub rule); no user-facing strings (no localization cascade). Changeset: share + deep-links at **minor** — fixed-lockstep bumps all 40 to 0.3.0, never major ([[oss_changeset_bump_lockstep]] landmine avoided; `check:changeset` OK, 30 pkgs covered). **Adversarial verification** (4-agent Workflow — 3 refute lenses [share action-values across iOS/Android/RN-versions · ref-count interleavings · snapshot semantics + alloc scope] → judge): verdict **clean**, zero residual defects. Gate green (lint/typecheck/test/build FULL TURBO / deps / changeset). SESSION_LOG #229 canonical.
- **OSS-B24 — render: shader-effect + z-order correctness** (2026-07-08, session #228). Twenty-third Arc-OSS row (engine; P1). `packages/render/src/{shaders.ts,frame-renderer.ts,sprite-batch.ts,camera2d.ts}` + `packages/react/src/game-view.tsx` + `docs/design/api-specs.md` + the render/react/render-web `tests/` + `.changeset/oss-b24-render-shader-zorder.md`. Five correctness fixes (spec §16 B24), **option A chosen at the approval stop** for the shader fork. **(1) water/fog/fire could not render** — the SkSL declared an unbound `uniform shader src` child (effects call `makeShader` with no children) and hardcoded resolution `[0]=[1]=0` (WATER `coord/resolution` → div-by-zero). Owner picked **drop `src`** (procedural overlays) over binding the child: the effects run through the batched `drawAtlas` path where one shared image-shader can't sample per-sprite, `RuntimeEffectLike` has no `makeShaderWithChildren`, api-specs:94 already describes them procedurally, and the 6 zone-bg siblings are all source-free. Dropped `uniform shader src` + every `src.eval` from FIRE/FOG/WATER; WATER returns `half4(final,1)`, FOG a `density*n` alpha overlay, FIRE a `flameMask` overlay; resolution now fed from `commands[0].dstW/dstH` (captured in `drawAtlas`, guarded `max(resolution,float2(1))`). **(2) frame time hardcoded 0** — `game-view.tsx:167` `beginFrame(w,h,0,pd)` → `ctx.loop.clock.elapsed` (public, zero-alloc), so time-driven shaders animate. **(3) z-order lost across buckets** — `SpriteBatch.flush()` globally bucketed by `(image,effect,alpha)` then drew in first-encounter order, discarding cross-texture z (RenderSystem z-sorts before queuing). Rewritten as **consecutive-run grouping** keyed `(image,effect,alpha,stretch)`, one reused scratch bucket (`_batches[0]`), zero-alloc in steady state; interleaved tex1,tex2,tex1 → 3 ordered calls. **(4) stretch dropped effects** — `stretch` added to the run key; the stretch branch now calls `resolveEffectPaint` (was alpha-only). **(5) dead-zone follow double-counted** `relX` — all four `camera2d.ts` branches fixed to `relX + offsetX - dz.x` form. **Adversarial multi-agent review (16 agents, 5 dimensions → per-finding refute-default verify): 7 confirmed / 4 refuted.** Confirmed + fixed in a second pass: outline+stretch rendered a flat silhouette (guarded `outline` back to plain stretchPaint — two-pass effect has no single-pass stretch form); the render-web conformance "non-zero pixels" test was a **false positive** (CPU CanvasKit `drawAtlas` does NOT rasterize `RuntimeEffect` — verified by driving a discriminating atlas color and reading back the raw atlas; the opaque-black clear also made `maxChannel` scan alpha=255) → re-scoped as an honest end-to-end smoke test (RGB-only scan, docstring corrected, real proof pointed at the `makeShader` uniform spy); added a frame-renderer `stretch:true`+effect test (the fix was untested — a revert stayed green) and fog/fire time-slot assertions (done-when's "animating time" was unproven for them); deleted dead `wave1`/`wave2` locals in WATER_SKSL. Refuted (correctly): api-specs:94 "still claims sampling" (it never did), the `!surface` guard (preceded by `expect().not.toBeNull()`), the `_batches` vestigial nit, and "visible regression for consumers" (the effects never rendered before). **Done-when amended for honesty** (`road-to-oss-2026-09.md:1267`): pixel-level shader execution is unobservable on CPU CanvasKit, so water/fog/fire correctness is proven by real-CanvasKit uniform spies (resolution slots [0]/[1]=dstW/dstH; time slot advances) + a non-throwing smoke readback, with GPU-visual validation deferred to the owner manual-test window. No exported symbol added/renamed (no react mock-stub rule triggered beyond adding `clock` to `MockLoop`); no user-facing strings (no localization cascade). Changeset: render **minor** (observable rendering behavior change) + react **patch** (internal time plumbing); fixed-lockstep → all 40 to 0.3.0, `changeset status` confirms zero major. Gate green (lint/typecheck/test 83 tasks/build 42 tasks/deps/changeset — all covered). SESSION_LOG #228 canonical.
- **OSS-B23 — input-validation + prototype-safety hardening batch** (2026-07-08, session #227). Twenty-second Arc-OSS row (engine; P1). `packages/{animation/src/sprite-animator.ts, audio/src/sfx-pool.ts, core/src/{game-loop.ts,clock.ts,coroutine.ts,app-lifecycle.ts}, dialogue/src/{graph.ts,runner.ts}, localization/src/{manager.ts,format.ts}}` + the 10 matching `tests/` files + `.changeset/oss-b23-*.md`. Ten sites across three classes, several reproduced live. **A · hang guards (throw on non-positive):** `sprite-animator.ts` `frameDuration <= 0` (infinite `while (elapsed >= frameDuration)` — matches the package's own `periodicTween`/step-sequence throws); `sfx-pool.ts` ctor `maxPolyphony <= 0` (busy-spin); `game-loop.ts` `fixedDt <= 0` guarded in BOTH ctor AND `step()` (the latter catches a post-ctor `clock.fixedDt = 0` mutation). Plain `new Error(...)` matching each package's existing style. **B · prototype-safe lookups (`Object.hasOwn`):** dialogue `graph.ts` `getNode('__proto__')` returned `Object.prototype` and soft-locked the runner (also un-blocks B10's `onError` on this path) + `runner.ts` `interpolate('{constructor}')` leaked the native `Function` string; localization `manager.ts` `has()`/`t()` + `format.ts` all FOUR `values[key]` accessors in `renderPlaceholder` (spec cited only one) leaked inherited members — all now `Object.hasOwn`-gated, so `__proto__`/`constructor`/`toString` return `undefined`/left-as-is and explicit own keys still work. **C · re-entrancy (live-index-iteration class):** `clock.ts` `tickSchedules` (undefined-guard + one-shot splice by `indexOf(entry)` not stale `i`, so a callback cancelling a lower-index entry can't double-fire the current one); `coroutine.ts` `update()` (undefined-guard, drop `!` — `clear()` inside a running coroutine no longer TypeErrors); `app-lifecycle.ts` `_inBackground` idempotency flag (a second `background()` no longer discards the resume flag → `foreground()` still resumes). **Stale spec lines corrected at HEAD** (re-verified 2026-07-08 vs 2026-07-03 spec): game-loop hang is `step()` :125 not :108; runner leak is :70 not :43; format needs 4 sites not 1; manager needs both `has()` and `t()`. **Tests:** +behavioral asserts extended into the 10 existing test files (no new files — avoids mock-pollution) — `toThrow` for 0 and negative inputs incl. the game-loop post-ctor-mutation path; prototype keys prove `undefined`/as-is + explicit-own-key still interpolates; three re-entrancy regression tests (callback-scheduled entry doesn't fire same tick + cancel-from-callback no double-fire; `clear()` mid-run no throw; double-`background()` still resumes + `onBackground` fires once). 311 tests across the 5 packages, 0 failures. **No user-facing strings (no localization cascade); no render/react export change (no react mock-stub rule).** Changeset: 5 packages (animation/audio/core/dialogue/localization) at **minor** — fixed-lockstep bumps all 40 to 0.3.0, `changeset status` confirmed zero major (the 1.0.0 landmine [[oss_changeset_bump_lockstep]] avoided). **Web-input scope-check (owner asked "are web inputs in the plan?"):** a 3-auditor + adversarial-verify workflow confirmed B23 touches ZERO web-input code by design ("input-validation" = argument-value + object-key validation, not the input subsystem), and the audit's "input-validation trap class" is fully covered by B23 alone. It also surfaced + verified THREE unowned siblings of the SAME classes outside B23's scope — deep-links URL query/param prototype read-leak (`parse.ts:76` + `router.ts:38`, CONFIRMED empirically), input-package hang-guards (`virtual-joystick.ts:74,77` + raw `gamepad.ts:82`), and `sources.ts:48` popstate iterate-during-dispatch (CONFIRMED). Owner chose **keep B23 as approved, file the gaps as a new row**: filed **OSS-B23b** (§17 + P2 todo pointer) for the deep-links + input-package sites, and folded the `sources.ts` re-entrancy angle into **B25**'s done-when. `postfx-surface.ts:185` NaN-resize refuted as a live bug (owned by C1 regardless). - **OSS-B19 — postfx: correctness slivers + surface scrub** (2026-07-08, session #224). Nineteenth Arc-OSS row (engine; P1). `packages/postfx/{src/runtime.ts, src/effect.ts, src/shaders.ts, src/controller.ts, tests/runtime.test.ts, tests/weather-effects.test.ts}` + `.changeset/oss-b19-*.md`. **(1) Array-uniform truncation:** `SkiaRuntimeEffectLike.makeShaderWithChildren`'s uniforms param was `ArrayLike<number>` — the type hole that let a caller pass a plain `number[]` some CanvasKit builds silently truncate past index 4 (render-web carried an `as Float32Array` workaround cast for exactly this). Narrowed to `Float32Array`; the in-repo `packUniforms` already returns Float32Array so runtime is unchanged, but >4-float uniform buffers (god-rays' 12) now reach the shader by construction. Regression test: pack god-rays → the shader mock captures a length-12 Float32Array (fails if reverted to ArrayLike<number> + a truncating number[]). **(2) Weather uniforms (owner: wire direction, remove speed):** the weather shaders are stateless (phase fed in via `scrollOffset*`; the rain SkSL comment says the caller advances it by `speed*dt`). `clouds.direction` was in the public type but absent from `WEATHER_CLOUDS_SKSL` (X-only scroll) — now WIRED: added `uniform float direction`, cloudUv scrolls along the rotated axis `scrollOffsetX*(cos,sin)/resolution` (direction=0 reproduces the previous pure-horizontal scroll exactly), packed at index 4 → new `weather-clouds` layout `[width,height,intensity,scrollOffsetX,direction]` (len 4→5). `rain.direction` was already wired (runtime.ts packs it, SkSL consumes it as rotation) — untouched. `speed` was a true silent no-op on both rain and clouds (declared, never packed, no SkSL counterpart; observable wiring needs a per-frame `time` uniform = B24's hardcoded-0 plumbing, out of scope) → **REMOVED** from `WeatherRainUniforms`/`WeatherCloudsUniforms` + both factories; the stale "B5-passthrough fields" comment rewritten. Test: two `direction` values yield different packed output for weather-clouds. **(3) Unsilenceable `console.info`:** `createPostFxRuntime(skia)`'s first-compile `console.info` diagnostic was unconditional. Added optional 2nd arg `{ verbose?: boolean }`; the info is gated behind `verbose` (compile-failure `console.warn` stays); all existing callers pass only `skia` → unaffected, released builds silent by default. Tests: fires once with `{verbose:true}`, silent by default even when `getUniformFloatCount` is present. **(4) Private-vocab scrub:** `controller.ts` doc comments leaked `Pan Tvardowski`/`Phase 11.14`/`PT createPanTvardowskiPostFx`/`session #196` into the public `.d.ts`; rewritten project-neutral. - **OSS-B17 — audio: dead `rate` + false auto-suspend** (2026-07-08, session #223). Eighteenth Arc-OSS row (engine; P1). `packages/audio/{src/sfx-pool.ts, src/audio-manager.ts, src/music.ts, README.md, tests/sfx-pool.test.ts}` + `docs/design/api-specs.md` + `.changeset/oss-b17-*.md`. **Dead `rate`:** `PlaySFXOptions.rate` was declared (`sfx-pool.ts:8`) but `play()` never read it. Implemented via the structural route: `AudioBufferSourceNodeLike` gains an optional `readonly playbackRate?: { value: number }` (Web-Audio shape — verified against the installed `react-native-audio-api`, whose `AudioBufferSourceNode.playbackRate` is a writable-`.value` `AudioParam`), and `play()` applies it with `const rateParam = source.playbackRate; if (rateParam && opts?.rate !== undefined) rateParam.value = opts.rate` — an **undefined**-guard (not falsy) so `rate: 0` is honored, a no-op when the injected node lacks the field, zero new per-call allocation on the hot path. **`onended` readonly-cast (findings ledger):** the interface's `onended` was made writable, so all three `(source as { onended })` casts collapse to a direct `source.onended = …` — one in `sfx-pool.ts` plus the two in `music.ts` (the latter owner-authorized at close, since they are the same findings-ledger item in the same package; the ledger's "package's own code casts to assign onended" is now fully closed). **False auto-suspend claim:** README:3 + api-specs both claimed automatic background/foreground suspend; **zero** `AppState`/`visibilitychange` wiring exists (suspend is manual, `audio-manager.ts`). Both claims removed; README gains a **Lifecycle (manual)** section (native `AppState` + web `visibilitychange` → `suspend()`/`resume()`), api-specs' claim replaced with an explicit "manual — no automatic listener" note, and the `suspend`/`resume` JSDoc reworded to "call from your own handler". **README example (owns the audio README end-state; Arc D skips it):** fixed all three wrong counts — `new AudioManager()` (was `new AudioManager(audioContext)`; ctor takes no args), `new SFXPool(8)` + `connect(ctx, channels.get('sfx')!.node)` (was `new SFXPool(audioContext, channels.sfx, { maxVoices: 8 })` — `channels` is a `Map`, `maxVoices` isn't an option), `manager.init(() => ctx)` sync factory (was `await manager.init()`); added register-before-play and the `rate` option. **Tests:** +4 (30→34) — rate applied when the node exposes `playbackRate` (fails old: was never read), `rate: 0` applied (locks the undefined-guard against a future `if (opts?.rate)` simplification that would silently drop it), `playbackRate` untouched when `rate` omitted (no-clobber), and no-throw + `activeCount===1` when the node lacks `playbackRate`. **Process:** 4-lens adversarial review (correctness × doc-honesty × test-rigor × spec-compliance) with refute-default verify — **0 confirmed defects, 0 source bugs, 0 scope violations**; three lenses independently confirmed leaving `music.ts` untouched is correct; the one material nit (rate=0 untested) was adopted as the +1 hardening test; the flagged music.ts dead casts were then owner-authorized and cleaned at close; the README `shootBuffer` placeholder (standard illustrative snippet) left by design. External surface independently confirmed: `react-native-audio-api` exports `AudioContext` + `decodeAudioData`. Changeset: audio **patch** (lockstep — additive optional `playbackRate`, `onended` widened to writable, `rate` now functional). Gate green (lint/typecheck/test 34/build FULL TURBO/deps/changeset). - **OSS-B16 — react: GameView per-frame churn** (2026-07-08, session #222). Seventeenth Arc-OSS row (engine; P1). `packages/react/{src/game-view.tsx, tests/game-view.test.ts}` + `.changeset/oss-b16-*.md`. **Churn:** `<GameView>`'s `onPreRender` called `setPasses(built)` unconditionally every frame with a fresh array from `bridge.buildPostFxPasses` (a fresh `[]` even for the empty chain), defeating React's bailout; it also re-created the `RuntimeShader` element array and allocated 4 inline responder handlers + a style array on every render — so a postfx-free game still re-rendered the canvas subtree each frame. **Fix:** a module-level `samePasses(a,b)` shallow signature gates `setPasses` via a closure `prevBuilt` (`if (!samePasses(prevBuilt, built)) { prevBuilt = built.length===0 ? EMPTY_PASSES : built; setPasses(prevBuilt) }`); the empty chain collapses to a shared module-level `EMPTY_PASSES` const (the `passes` state is seeded with it, so a postfx-free mount never re-renders from passes); the `RuntimeShader` element array is `useMemo`'d on `[passes]`, the 4 handlers `useCallback`'d on `[ctx]`, the container style `useMemo`'d on `[style]`. `setPicture`-per-frame kept (the RN-Skia render model). No API/export change (index.ts untouched). **Owner-approved deviation from the row's spec text** ("length + per-pass id + shader ref"): the signature ALSO compares uniform values + child-image refs, because the hit-flash decay and timed triggers animate uniforms while holding `id`/`source` constant (`postfx/controller.ts` `update()`) — an id+source-only gate would freeze those animations, violating the row's own "no behavior change to postfx output". Proven: with an id+source-only signature the flash-decay + vector-uniform tests fail (payload frozen at frame 1). **Tests:** extended `game-view.test.ts` in place (no new file — avoids the known cross-file mock-pollution); a controllable `_buildPasses` feeds `bridge.buildPostFxPasses` per tick, `_setPassesCount` counts the passes-setter invocations, `_lastPasses` captures the pushed value. Render-count fail-old/pass-new (empirically confirmed: **old unconditional → 5 fail; id+source-only → 2 fail**): unchanged non-empty chain fires once across N frames, empty chain fires zero, changed uniform/vector/source-ref/id/length each propagate, steady stretches silent. **Adversarial 4-lens review (find→verify, 11 agents) — zero source bugs, 2 confirmed test-coverage gaps (5 refuted), both fixed + fail-injection-verified:** (1) the passes-setter mock discarded its argument, so tests proved WHEN `setPasses` fired but never WITH WHAT — a `setPasses(EMPTY_PASSES)`-instead-of-`prevBuilt` regression stayed green; now the mock captures the payload and tests assert the propagated id/uniforms + that the empty-collapse pushes one stable `EMPTY_PASSES` ref. (2) `sameChildren` had no coverage (all `makePass` calls defaulted `children:[]`); added a ref/length-varying tick that fails if `sameChildren` is broken. 25 tests (was 22 + 3). Changeset: react **patch** (no API change; lockstep). Gate green (lint/typecheck/test 25/build/deps/changeset). SESSION_LOG #222 canonical.
- **OSS-B15 — ui: reactive focus** (2026-07-07, session #221). Sixteenth Arc-OSS row (engine; P1). `packages/ui/{src/focus-context.ts, src/focus-provider.tsx, src/button.tsx, src/index.ts, tests/primitives.test.ts}` + `.changeset/oss-b15-*.md`. **Bug:** `FocusProvider`'s only reactivity was a `force((n)=>n+1)` counter that re-rendered the provider itself, but `children` is a reference-identical element so React bailed out — consumers never re-rendered; `Button` read `manager.getFocused()` at render with no subscription, so focus rings never repainted (the README keyboard-nav claim was visually dead). **Fix:** context now carries `FocusContextValue { manager, focusedId }` (was bare `FocusManager | null`); `FocusProvider` holds `focusedId` state seeded from `mgr.getFocused()`, its effect resyncs (child auto-focus effects fire before the parent effect) then `subscribe`s driving `setFocusedId`, and publishes a `useMemo`-stable value — the force counter is gone. `useFocusManager()` keeps its `FocusManager | null` signature (now reads `.manager`); new exported `useFocusedId()` hook; `Button` derives `focused = useFocusedId() === id` (reactive). **Tests:** the 12 `typeof`-shell primitive tests replaced with 5 behavioral ones (self-contained stateful-hook mock with per-context routing — `createContext._current` + `useContext(ctx)=>ctx._current` so `useTheme`/`useFocusedId` resolve independently — modeled on `react/overlay-hooks.test.ts`): provider republishes a new value on focus move (fail-old — the counter never changed the published value), Button ring `borderWidth`/`selected` follow `context.focusedId`, press focuses via `manager.focus(id)` + fires `onPress`, disabled ignores press, register/activate/dispose. 33 pass. **Out of scope (owners):** `register()` auto-focus removal = B28; the arrow/tab input driver = M1b. Changeset: ui **minor** (additive `useFocusedId`/`FocusContextValue`; `FocusContext` generic widened — direct-context consumers affected, `FocusProvider` users unaffected; lockstep). now each reads `version` from its own `package.json` (named JSON import — inlined by tsup at build, live under bun at test), so `flare --version`/`create-flare-app --version` print the real version and `{{FLARE_VERSION}}` stamps `^<version>`. `create-flare-app` hand-bumped `0.0.0→0.2.1` and joined the changeset `fixed` group (`["@flare-engine/*","create-flare-app"]`) so H1 aligns it to the lockstep minor (stays `private:true`). **(2) Windows spawn + injection guard:** `runCreateFlareApp` (cli) and `runInstall` (scaffold) used `${cmd}.cmd` + `shell:false` (EINVAL on Node ≥20.12) → replaced with `spawn(cmd, args, { shell: process.platform==='win32' })` via pure exported `createAppSpawnSpec`/`installSpawnSpec` helpers, and the app name is validated `/^[a-z0-9@/_-]+$/i` (exported `isValidAppName`) before any shell-mode spawn — in the `flare new` action and at the top of `scaffoldApp`. **(3) tiled-to-flare default out:** `join(dirname(inputPath), inputPath.replace(...))` double-prepended the already-absolute input (`/maps/maps/…`, invalid on a Windows drive) → ENOENT without `-o`; fixed to `basename(inputPath)`. **(4) `--no-embed-tilesets`:** emitted an all-zeros stub that dropped the external ref; added optional `source?: string` to `FlareTileset` and carry `ref.source` through when `!embed`. **(5) Template dotfiles:** npm strips `.gitignore` from the published tarball (proven via `bun pm pack --dry-run`), so all three templates' `.gitignore`→`gitignore` and the scaffolder renames it back on copy. **Tests:** vacuous `__APP_NAME__`/`__TEMPLATE_ID__` negatives replaced with the real `{{…}}` token syntax (now catch un-replaced tokens); The `flare` CLI bin was already fine (no top-level `import.meta.url`) — untouched. **Test:** new `bin.test.ts` builds the package in `beforeAll` (the gate runs `test` before `build`, so it can't assume a fresh `dist/`) then EXECUTES both `dist/bin.js` (CJS) and `dist/bin.mjs` (ESM) with `--version`, asserting the real version prints and neither crashes — fails old (CJS throws), passes new. **Verified E2E:** the CJS bin now scaffolds a full shmup app (templates resolved via the shimmed dir, `.gitignore` written, `^0.2.1` stamped). `create-flare-app` is `private:true` → no changeset. Gate green. SESSION_LOG #218 canonical.
- **OSS-B12 — iap-revenuecat: money-path fixes** (2026-07-07, session #217). Thirteenth Arc-OSS row (engine; **P0 money-path**). `packages/iap-revenuecat/{src/provider.ts, src/types.ts, package.json, tests}`. Six fixes to the RevenueCat `IAPProvider` adapter. **(1) Restore active-only:** `mapRestoreResults` mapped `entitlements.all` (includes EXPIRED → re-granted lapsed subs); extended the structural `RevenueCatCustomerInfo` with `entitlements.active` and now maps that (`nonSubscriptionTransactions` kept — one-time, no expiry). **(2) Listener lifecycle (leak + null-deref):** `onTransaction`'s unsubscribe was broken both ways — a pre-resolution unsub no-op'd while the pending `.then` still added the listener (leak), and a post-add unsub nulled `listener` synchronously before the async `removeCustomerInfoUpdateListener` dereferenced it → `remove(null)`. Rewrote with a `cancelled` flag (pre-resolution unsub suppresses the pending add) + the real listener captured in a closure `const` (removal never sees null) + idempotent double-unsub. **(3) Emit all entitlements:** the listener emitted only `entitlements[0]`; now iterates and emits every ACTIVE entitlement. **(4) Honest tx id:** `purchase()` labelled `originalPurchaseDate` (a date string) as the transaction `id`; now prefers the real `transactionIdentifier` from the matching `nonSubscriptionTransactions` entry (reverse-scan by productId) with `purchasedAt` from its `purchaseDateMillis`, and a documented date fallback only when the store surfaces no tx id. **(5) `onError` hook:** `getProducts`/`restore` swallowed all errors into `[]`; added optional `onError?(err)` on `RevenueCatConfig`, called in both catch blocks (default still returns `[]`, now observable). **(6) getProducts category (§16 audit):** no category was passed → RN-purchases defaults to SUBSCRIPTION → Android one-time products returned an empty catalog + `purchase()` "Product not found". Owner-chosen approach = **configurable**: new `productCategory?: string` on config passed verbatim as the getProducts category/type arg (no SDK-enum guessing; caller supplies the constant for their RN-purchases version), applied to both `getProducts` and `purchase`'s internal lookup. **(7) Peers (§16):** dropped the phantom `react` peer (never imported) and declared `react-native >=0.79.0` (the undeclared `await import('react-native')`); C1 folds this into its RN-peer matrix. **Tests:** +8 (8→16 across the two files) — category pass-through, expired-not-re-granted, onError×2, real-tx-id, unsub-before-resolution (no leak), double-unsub idempotent, emit-all-active; updated the getProducts call-args assertion + moved the integration restore mock's entitlements to `.active`. **NOT** the configure-once race (OSS-B27). Changeset: iap-revenuecat **minor** (new `productCategory`/`onError` config API + peer changes). Gate green (lint/typecheck/test 16/build; deps + changeset OK); frictions none. - **OSS-B11 — physics + input: dup-add + pointer delta** (2026-07-07, session #216). Twelfth Arc-OSS row (engine; P1). `packages/physics/{src/collision-world.ts, tests/collision-world.test.ts}` + `packages/input/{src/pointer.ts, tests/pointer.test.ts}`. **Broadphase leak:** `CollisionWorld.add` unconditionally `spatial.insert`-ed even for a re-added entity (`collision-world.ts:76-79`), so re-adding left a stale duplicate record in the brute-force `SpatialIndex` (whose `remove`/`update` only touch the first match) → unbounded broadphase growth + wasted narrowphase checks. Fixed with an `entries.has(entity)` branch routing an existing entity to `spatial.update` (in place, zero-alloc; the new-entity path stays byte-identical). Collision **outcomes** were already safe — `step()` dedups pairs via a Set and re-checks narrowphase against the Map's live coords, so a stale record can't fabricate a wrong collision — meaning the defect was pure record leak; a new documented `get broadphaseSize()` accessor (returns `spatial.size`, mirroring the existing `SpatialIndex.size`) makes the count observable for the regression test (additive API). **Pointer delta:** `pointerMove`/`pointerDown`/`pointerUp` overwrote `delta` with `= (x − position)`, so multiple events in one frame kept only the **last** segment — contradicting the `pointer.ts:7` "delta since last frame" contract. Changed all three to `+=` (accumulate the segment before updating position); `resetPointerFrame` already zeroes delta at the frame boundary so accumulation resets per-frame. All 6 existing pointer tests are single-event-per-frame off a zeroed delta → behavior-identical (verified). **Tests:** physics +2 (68→70: re-add keeps `broadphaseSize===1` [fails old: 2] + collisions-stay-correct after a moving re-add), input +2 (59→61: two moves/frame sum to 15 [fails old: 5] + move-then-up accumulates to 10 [fails old: 5]). Pre-existing suites unchanged-green. Changeset: physics **minor** (new `broadphaseSize`) + input **patch** (delta fix), lockstep. Gate green (lint/typecheck/test 70+61/build FULL TURBO; deps + changeset OK); frictions none (verify.sh run once — the fix-loop validated with raw commands to avoid the duplicate-block friction from #215). SESSION_LOG #216 canonical.
- **OSS-B10 — dialogue: dangling ids + cycles** (2026-07-07, session #215). Eleventh Arc-OSS row (engine; P1). `packages/dialogue/{src/runner.ts, src/index.ts, tests/runner.test.ts}`. **Dangling id:** `processNode` silently `end()`-ed on a transition to a missing node id (`runner.ts:93-96`) — authors got no diagnostic. Added a typed `DialogueError extends Error` (`code: 'missing-node' | 'cycle'`, plus `nodeId`, `missingId?`, `chain?`; matches the repo's `TilemapParseError` convention) and an optional `onError(err)` hook on `DialogueRunnerOptions`. A missing target now builds a `missing-node` error naming the graph, the missing id, and the **source** node (tracked via the walk's `from`), routed to `onError` when supplied else `console.warn` — the conversation still ends (outcome unchanged, now visible). **Cycles:** the mutually-recursive `processNode`/`processBranch`/`processAction` chain overflowed the stack (`RangeError`) on a content cycle (`runner.ts:164,:179`). Collapsed into one iterative `run(entryId, fromNodeId)` loop with `STEP_BUDGET = 1000` — branch/action transitions advance in-loop, line/choice pause (emit + return), end/null terminate; exceeding the budget throws a typed `DialogueError('cycle')` naming the last 12 visited ids in `chain` (no more `RangeError`). `start`/`advance`/`selectChoice` rewired to `run` with the null-next guards preserved at each call site; `processLine`/`processChoice` unchanged. Re-traced every existing path (line/choice/branch/fallback/action/null-next/missing-next/end) — identical observable behavior. **Index:** `DialogueError` (value) + `DialogueErrorCode` (type) added to the explicit barrel. **Out of scope (deferred):** the `__proto__`/`{constructor}` prototype-key leaks on `graph.ts:98`/`runner.ts:43` (§1226-1228) → **B23**, which owns the `Object.hasOwn` guards; B10's `onError` fires correctly for ordinary missing ids. **Tests:** +3 (dialogue 42→45) — `onError` receives the typed error with correct `code`/`nodeId`/`missingId` and still ends; default `console.warn` fallback verified via `spyOn`; an `A→B→A` two-branch cycle throws `DialogueError` `code:'cycle'` and is provably **not** a `RangeError`. Existing suite untouched-green. Changeset: dialogue **minor** (new `DialogueError`/`DialogueErrorCode` exports + `onError` option are additive API; lockstep). Gate green (lint/typecheck/test 45/build; deps + changeset OK). **Friction:** verify.sh appends a SESSION_LOG block per invocation → the fix-loop's 4 gate re-runs left 4 duplicate blocks (215–218), collapsed to one. SESSION_LOG #215 canonical.
- **OSS-B9 — deep-links: scheme-URL parsing** (2026-07-07, session #214). Tenth Arc-OSS row (engine; P1). `packages/deep-links/{src/parse.ts, README.md, tests}`. **Bug:** `parseUrl` treated the first token after `://` as `host` for EVERY scheme, so an RN deep link `myapp://zone/4` → host `zone`, segments `['4']` and never matched `/zone/:id` (`DeepLinkRouter.match` routes on `segments`+`query` only, never `host`). **Fix:** only `http`/`https` extract an authority into `host`; any other scheme → `host=''` and the **entire post-`://` remainder is the path** (`myapp://zone/4` → segments `['zone','4']`). A scheme-detection guard (`/^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9+.-]*$/` on the prefix) stops a `://` inside a query (`/redirect?to=http://x.com`) being read as a scheme; the http authority now stops at the first `/?#` (fixes a pre-existing bug that folded a query-only universal link's query into `host`). `host` is now a documented tri-state — real authority (http/https) · `''` (custom scheme) · `null` (relative) — pinned in the `ParsedUrl` JSDoc; dropped the now-false "ignores username/password" docstring claim. **README:** fixed the `new DeepLinkManager(router, source)` 2-arg call (didn't typecheck — real ctor takes `{ router, source }`) and the stateless example `flare://game/zone/4` (the `game` token was a relic of the host-swallow bug — post-fix it's a 3rd segment that wouldn't match `/zone/:id`) → `flare://zone/4` with annotated output. **Tests:** rewrote the 2 tests that codified the host-swallow bug (flipped expectations to `host===''` + full-remainder segments); added custom→path, http→host, http no-path, query-only regression, scheme-guard, and an explicit `''`-vs-`null` tri-state lock; **added 2 router end-to-end scheme tests** (`router.test.ts` never fed a `://` URL before) proving `myapp://zone/4` and `https://example.com/zone/4` both route `/zone/:id` → `id==='4'`. parse 7→13, router 8→10 (28 total across 3 files; `manager.test.ts` confirmed unaffected). **Process:** a 4-agent **pre-task deep audit** (19 findings) shaped the design before implementation — caught the README example self-contradiction, the tri-state contract, the http query-fold bug, and confirmed router/manager tests unaffected; the post-implementation 4-lens adversarial review (2-skeptic verify) then returned **zero findings**. **Deferred (owners):** `api-specs.md` scheme-shape note → D5; `sources.ts` `createWebSource` → B25; userinfo/port/IPv6/backslash normalization → out of scope (router ignores `host`). Changeset: deep-links **patch** (behavior-correcting fix; body notes custom-scheme route patterns must now include the formerly-swallowed segment). Gate green (lint/typecheck/test 28/build; deps + changeset OK); frictions none. SESSION_LOG #214 canonical.
- **OSS-B8 — tilemap: GID flip bits + LDtk pipeline** (2026-07-07, session #213). Ninth Arc-OSS row (engine; P0; 2-session). `packages/tilemap/{src,tests}`. **Tiled flip bits:** `GID_FLIP_MASK = 0x1fffffff` + `stripGidFlags`/`decodeGidFlags(): TileFlipFlags` (high-bit H/V/D; `!== 0`, not `> 0`, since `gid & 0x80000000` is a negative int32). Masked at all four GID consumers — `getTilesetForGid`+`getTileSourceRect` (mask internally), collider `localId` (+ stores the masked gid), renderer UV `localId`. Flipped tiles previously resolved to the LAST tileset and lost solid flags; now resolve correctly and keep colliders. Render-time flip *transforms* deferred to M10 (documented). **LDtk pipeline restructured to two passes:** pass 1 collects `Tiles`/`AutoLayer` `__tilesetDefUid`s, **sorts uids ascending**, assigns deterministic monotonic `firstGid`s (map.tilesets stays ascending for `getTilesetForGid`'s early-break); pass 2 resolves each tile as `firstGid(layerUid) + t` (was `t+1`, all tiles landed in tileset #1) with a `?? 1` fallback for a referenced-but-undefined uid. **IntGrid → synthetic collision tileset:** cells `>0` remap to `firstGid + (v-1)` (identity at firstGid=1, so the raw-`[1,0,1,0,1,0]` test holds), populating `solidTiles` via a configurable predicate (`ParseLDtkOptions.intGridSolid`, default `v>0`); `extractColliders` now returns non-empty correct colliders for LDtk maps. **Parser honesty:** new `TilemapParseError`; `parseTiled` throws actionable errors on infinite/base64-compressed-chunked/external-tileset/non-orthogonal maps; `parseLDtk` on non-project input + `externalLevels`. Real two-tileset fixtures (`tests/fixtures/`) with H/V/D-flipped tiles (unsigned form) + non-contiguous uids (1,7) + IntGrid walls. **Adversarial 6-lens review (14 agents, find→2×-skeptic-verify) caught 3 real defects the green gate missed, 0 false positives:** (1) `parseLayer` routed any non-`tilelayer` (real Tiled `group`/`imagelayer`) to `parseObjectLayer` → raw `TypeError` instead of `TilemapParseError` — made dispatch explicit (tilelayer/objectgroup, else throw); (2) the LDtk "core regression" test was tautological (each tile self-resolves to its own tileset regardless of the uid-sort) — strengthened to assert stable firstGid VALUES (`TileB.firstGid===17`, stored gid `20`) that break when the sort is dropped; (3) the renderer's flip-mask consumer (one of the 4 spec-mandated) had ZERO coverage — every renderer test used gid=1/firstGid=1 and asserted only `batch.count`; added a two-tileset test capturing real `cmd.srcX/srcY` that fails (srcY 8589934592) if the mask is removed. Plus fixture fidelity: flipped GIDs authored as real unsigned Tiled values (`+ 0x80000000`, not JS-negative `|`). Tests: 49→72. Changeset: tilemap **minor** (new API). Gate green (lint/typecheck/test 72/build; deps + changeset OK); **gate:1** — builder didn't re-run biome on `src` (multi-line import/`Array.isArray` format), caught by the gate's lint, `--write`-fixed. SESSION_LOG #213 canonical.
- **OSS-B7 — core + particles: loop alpha, pool caps, seeds** (2026-07-07, session #212). Eighth Arc-OSS row (engine; P1). Spans `packages/core` + `packages/particles` (particles wraps `core.Pool`, so core landed first). **GameLoop.alpha:** new `get alpha()` = `accumulator / clock.fixedDt`, guarded by a `_hasFixedDt` flag → returns exact 0 (no divide) when no fixed step configured; api-specs physics-interpolation note added. **core.Pool:** new `{ capacity }` ctor option + `tryAcquire(): T|null` (returns null at `_live >= capacity`, zero-alloc, no factory call) + a `__DEV__`-only double-release guard (`_liveSet`, never allocated in prod) + a `live` getter. `acquire()` semantics UNCHANGED (pop-or-factory, never throws, uncapped — only gains `_live++`); the `_liveSet?.delete` short-circuits in prod. `Pool.clear()` deliberately does not reset `_live` (documented). **particles:** `ParticlePool` passes `{ capacity }` to the inner Pool (cap==prewarm) + exposes `tryAcquire`; `ParticleEmitter` ctor changed to an **options-bag** `(config, { maxParticles?, seed? })` (**breaking — owner-sanctioned, full consumer control pre-OSS**; 2 tests + 3 benchmark call sites updated), `_emitOne()` now self-gates via `tryAcquire` (removed the 2 dead `config.maxParticles` gates), LCG seed injectable + `reseed`, default 1. **Adversarial 4-lens review (16 agents) caught 2 in-scope bugs the green gate missed:** (1) Park-Miller seed degeneracy — `{seed:0}`/`reseed(0)`/`2147483647`/negative seeds silently produced a dead or out-of-range RNG; fixed with a `_setSeed` normalizer into [1, 2147483646] that leaves valid seeds byte-identical (+3 tests). (2) the no-`fixedDt` alpha test drained the accumulator to exactly 0 so it passed even with the `_hasFixedDt` guard reverted; strengthened to `step(0.5/60)`. **Deferred to OSS-B23 (which explicitly owns `game-loop.ts:46/108 fixedDt<=0`):** `fixedDt<=0` → NaN alpha + infinite `step()` loop, and negative-`timescale` → alpha<0 — not silently fixed here to avoid conflicting with B23's validate-in-ctor+throw decision. Refuted: a `Pool.clear()`/`_live` "tryAcquire lockout" (documented contract; `ParticlePool.clear()` releases per-particle so `_live` returns to 0). Tests: core 73→88, particles 15→36. Changeset: core + particles **minor** (new API), with a breaking-ctor note (lockstep). Gate green (lint/typecheck/test/build; deps + changeset OK). SESSION_LOG #212 canonical.
- **OSS-B6 — storage: import safety + migration honesty** (2026-07-07, session #211). Seventh Arc-OSS row (engine; P1). `packages/storage/src/{storage.ts,migrations.ts}`. **importJSON atomicity:** `adapter.clear()` (storage.ts:132) ran BEFORE the per-entry `typeof v !== 'string'` check, so a payload with a bad entry value wiped storage before validation failed. Reordered so the complete payload is validated first — top-level shape guard (unchanged) THEN a full pass asserting every entry value is a string — and only then `clear()` + write. A bad payload now throws with storage untouched (validation reads the parsed in-memory object only; no adapter reads, no async reorder; the write loop re-iterates with `v as string` — a narrowing cast after the guard, not `any`). **runMigrations honesty:** the doc comment (migrations.ts:13-15) promised throw-on-gap + throw-when-highest-exceeds-`to`, but the impl only checked positive-integer + duplicates and silently skipped gaps. Added a gap check in the sorted pass (`m.version !== sorted[i-1].version + 1` → throw naming both bounding versions) and a ceiling check after the loop (guarded `length > 0`; `highest > to` → throw naming highest + target), matching the plain-`Error` style and `defineMigrationChain`'s existing contiguity semantics. The `from===to`/`from>to` early-exits are before the new checks, so no false positive on no-op/backward calls. +3 tests (storage 101→104: corrupt-payload-leaves-prior-data-untouched proves atomicity fail-old/pass-new; gap + ceiling throw tests assert on message content). Out of scope (deferred per spec): mid-write I/O-failure rollback (pre-existing), stricter-than-documented migration checks (e.g. a gap between `from` and the lowest provided migration). **Adversarial 3-lens review (find→verify workflow) ran clean — zero findings.** Changeset: storage patch (lockstep). Gate green (lint/typecheck/test 104/build, deps + changeset OK). SESSION_LOG #211 canonical.
- **OSS-B5 — scene: restart + async preload** (2026-07-06, session #210). Sixth Arc-OSS row (engine; P0). `packages/scene/src/scene-manager.ts`. **Restart bug:** `go(sameScene)` fetched the cached instance via `_getOrCreate` BEFORE destroying it, so the "fresh" mount reactivated the destroyed instance with its stale `World`/`Camera2D` (both instant and transition paths). Reordered so eviction/destroy happens first: instant path `_exitAndDestroyCurrent()` → then `_getOrCreate` builds fresh; transition path deletes the target from `_scenes`+`_initialized` up front so the fade constructs a fresh instance (new `World()`+`Camera2D()` via `_getOrCreate`:147-148). **Async-preload bug:** the transition path called `pending.onPreload?.()` WITHOUT await (entering before assets resolved), inconsistent with the instant path's `_initIfNeeded` await. Now preload starts concurrently with the fade-out, tracked by a new `_preloadResolved` flag, and `_updateTransition` holds at end-of-'out' until it resolves before `onInit`/`onEnter`. **Diagnostic:** `go(unknownName)` was a silent no-op → now a `__DEV__`-gated `console.warn` naming the scene + registered names (ambient `declare const __DEV__`; no new package dep — scene stays off the Tooling tier). **Adversarial 4-lens review (find→verify workflow, 12 agents) caught 2 latent bugs the green gate missed, both introduced by this row's own changes:** (1) restart-via-transition dropped the just-entered scene from `_initialized` — the switch's destroy loop deletes `pending.name` (s.name === pending.name on a same-name restart) and only `_scenes` was restored — so a later `push(sameScene)` re-ran `onPreload`/`onInit` on the live instance (onInit contract = once); fixed with `_initialized.add(pending.name)` at the switch (idempotent for the normal cross-scene case). (2) `void preload.then(onFulfilledOnly)` swallowed an `onPreload` rejection into a permanent soft-lock (`_preloadResolved` stuck false → transition hangs forever) plus an unhandled rejection; fixed with a two-arg `.then` whose reject arm sets `_preloadResolved = true` (unblock) and dev-logs `console.error`. +11 tests (scene 35→46: restart ×3, async-preload ×2, dev-warning ×2, + 2 regression locks written fail-old/pass-new for the review bugs). Out of scope (deferred post-flip, per spec): pause/resume, push/pop transitions, go-while-transitioning re-entrancy. Changeset: scene patch (lockstep). Gate green (lint/typecheck/test 46/46/build, deps + changeset OK). SESSION_LOG #210 canonical.
- **OSS-B4 — render: camera-rotation contract** (2026-07-06, session #209). Fifth Arc-OSS row (engine; P0). `Camera2D.rotation` is radians but both Skia backends (RN-Skia `SkCanvas`, CanvasKit) take **degrees**, so nonzero camera rotation rendered ~57× too small (radians treated as degrees). **Decision: `CanvasLike`/`DrawSurfaceLike.rotate` contract = DEGREES, pass-through everywhere; convert once at the camera seam.** `renderer.ts:91` `applyCamera` → `c.rotate((-rotation * 180) / Math.PI, 0, 0)` (the negation is the camera-inverts-world convention — a sprite at world-rotation R through camera-rotation C composes to R−C, consistent with shapes' object-direct `+rot`). Interface params `radians`→`degrees` on both `CanvasLike` (renderer.ts:7) and `DrawSurfaceLike` (frame-renderer.ts:121, doc "always in radians. Web shims must convert." dropped); the frame-renderer CanvasLike→DrawSurfaceLike adapter + both render-web bridges forward verbatim (now correct — deleted the "dormant mismatch" comments). api-specs:295's false "web module converts radians→degrees" claim corrected. **Grep confirmed exactly one conversion, no double-conversion; `shapes` is independent (imports `SkCanvas` from RN-Skia, not render's `CanvasLike`, already emits degrees — untouched reference).** +4 tests: 2 render unit (applyCamera(π/2)→≈-90; rotation=0 skips rotate) + **render-web CPU-surface readback parity** (drives `Renderer.applyCamera(π/2)` over a wrapped CanvasKit surface, reads pixels, asserts the red bar lands in the rotated quadrant and is absent at +x). The readback test **empirically failed against the old (radians) render dist and passes against the new (degrees) dist** — genuine fail-old/pass-new. **3-lens adversarial verify all-clean** (sign/direction correct + bit-exact -90, exactly one conversion, no consumer encodes old behavior — react game-view feeds radians, web-surface.test asserts verbatim pass-through). Changeset: render + render-web patch (lockstep). Gate green (42 turbo tasks, deps + changeset OK). SESSION_LOG #209 canonical.
- **OSS-B3 — share: screenshot format codes** (2026-07-06, session #208). Fourth Arc-OSS row (engine; P0 money-adjacent). **`capture.ts`:** `FORMAT_CODES` `{png:3, jpeg:2}` → `{png:4, jpeg:3}` — the old codes made a `png` request emit JPEG-encoded bytes (code 3 = ImageFormat.JPEG) mislabeled as png, and a `jpeg` request pass code 2 (not a valid ImageFormat member → Skia fell back to PNG). Now matches RN-Skia `ImageFormat {JPEG=3, PNG=4, WEBP=6}` (2.4.18); applied on both `encodeToBytes` and `encodeToBase64` paths. **`share.ts`:** `canShare` derived the share filename as hardcoded `screenshot.png` even for jpeg — now `screenshot.${format}`, matching `share()`. **README:** wrong payload field `image: shot.bytes` → `screenshot: shot` (+ `if (shot)` null guard for the `Screenshot | null` return) and a PT-vocab message scrubbed. +3 tests (share 22→25): a byte-code test (fake captures the code passed to `encodeToBytes`), a **contract test** that reads the installed `@shopify/react-native-skia` `ImageFormat` `.d.ts` and asserts the mapping (fakes can't catch a wrong enum; fails loud on a dep bump), and a `canShare` extension test (bun's real `File`, filename captured via `nav.canShare`). **3-lens adversarial verify:** fix byte-correct + complete (sole `png/jpeg`→Skia mapping in `packages/**`), all new tests confirmed genuine fail-old, canShare/share filename+mime now identical, README type-checks; surfaced an out-of-scope PT-vocab leak in `screenshot-scene.ts:10` → **deferred to OSS-B22** (its systematic `packages/*/src` grep will catch it). B25's `!== 'dismissed'` native-dispatched bug left untouched (its own row). Changeset: share patch (lockstep). Gate green (42 turbo tasks, deps + changeset OK). SESSION_LOG #208 canonical.
- **OSS-B2 — ecs: query dispose + entity iteration** (2026-07-05, session #207). Third Arc-OSS row (engine; ecs core + testing/devtools consumers). **ecs `World`:** `removeQuery(q)` (unregister), `forEachEntity(cb)` (live archetype-key walk, no array snapshot), `entityCount` getter. **testing:** `serializeScene` now memoizes one singleton query per (world, component) via a module-level `WeakMap` instead of registering N+1 permanent queries every call, and drops the redundant ALL query (union of singletons ⊇ ALL — snapshot identical). **devtools:** `useEntityInspector` walks `forEachEntity` instead of casting to a private `nextId` + id-range scan. README 32-cap corrected (module-global, not per-world). **3-lens adversarial self-verify caught a real bug in my first pass:** `removeQuery` called from inside a Query signal handler spliced `this.queries` mid-iteration in the `add`/`remove`/`destroy` notify-loops → silently skipped the next query (the natural one-shot self-dispose pattern triggers it). Fixed with the same deferral pattern B1 used for Signals (`queryIterDepth` counter + lazy `pendingQueryRemove`, zero hot-path alloc) + a regression test empirically confirmed fail-old; also documented `forEachEntity`'s spawn-in-callback footgun and fixed a misleading disposal comment. +8 tests (ecs 80→87, testing +1 memo regression, devtools +2 new hooks test via a self-contained `useContext` mock, no pollution). Changeset: ecs minor (new API) + testing/devtools patch (lockstep). Gate green (42 turbo tasks, deps + changeset OK). SESSION_LOG #207 canonical.
- **OSS-B1 — events: emit hardening** (2026-07-05, session #206). Second Arc-OSS row (engine). `Signal` + `EventEmitter`: boolean `iterating` flag → integer `emitDepth` counter with `try/finally`, so (1) a throwing handler no longer wedges deferred removal (finally always decrements + flushes at depth 0), and (2) a nested/re-entrant `emit()` no longer flushes the outer emit's `pendingRemove` early or mutates the live set mid-iteration. `remove`/`off` defer while `emitDepth > 0`; extracted `flushPending()` (cold path); dropped the depth reset from `clear()`/`removeAll()` (finally keeps it balanced). Kept live-Set iteration (added-during-emit runs same emit — no snapshot alloc); emit signatures + zero-alloc hot path unchanged; semantics now documented on both classes + README. **3-lens adversarial self-verify caught a pre-existing bug beyond the 2-file scope:** `removeAll(event)` mid-emit unconditionally wiped the *shared* `pendingRemove`, silently dropping deferred removals queued for *other* events — fixed (only the no-arg form clears it) + regression test; also tightened a re-entrancy doc nit. +7 tests (events 27→34), each written fail-old/pass-new and empirically confirmed to fail the old impl. Patch changeset added (lockstep). Gate green (42 turbo tasks, deps + changeset OK). SESSION_LOG #206 canonical.

- **Zone-1 polish — player FX / hit+kill feedback / bomb / boss HP** (2026-06-07, session #182). Owner-requested gameplay/FX pass before Phase 2; 7 deliberate behavior changes, all game-side (zero `packages/**`), owner-verified on A54. (1) Removed the player-ship sigil-opacity pulse (`sigilTween`/`playerSigilOpacity` had no HUD consumer — ship now constant alpha 1.0; unrelated BootOverlay `sigilOpacity()` kept). (2) Player hit by enemy bullet now shakes ×1 (amp 6/dur 0.2). (3) Player↔ship collision shakes ×2 (amp 12/0.4); damage expressed as `PLAYER_HP_MAX*0.1` (=10, value unchanged). `pendingShake` generalized boolean→`'x1'|'x2'|null` (stronger-wins, reset/frame; reduced-motion red-flash fallback intact; direct boss/miniboss/player-death shakes unchanged). Added 8 pure methods to the `Color` static namespace in `packages/math/src/color.ts`: `toOklab`/`fromOklab`/`toOklch`/`fromOklch` (canonical Ottosson constants, `Math.cbrt` not `**(1/3)` so negative LMS stays real; shared module-private `srgbToLinear`/`linearToSrgb` at the 0.04045 threshold — numerically identical to the older WCAG 0.03928 over all 8-bit inputs, so one linearizer serves both OKLab and WCAG), `deltaEOk(Color,Color)` + **`deltaEOkLab(Oklab,Oklab)`** (the +1 over the literal row — lets KG9's k-means pre-convert pixels once and inline the `Math.hypot` L2 distance instead of re-running `toOklab` per comparison), `relativeLuminance`, `contrastRatio`. New exported `Oklab`/`Oklch` types (`h` in degrees [0,360); `index.ts` re-exports both). **Edge cases:** achromatic guard `C<1e-7→h=0` (gray's cbrt rounding else reports a spurious ~88° hue), out-of-gamut clamp-after-delinearize (no NaN), `fromOklch` hue wrap `((h%360)+360)%360`, alpha drop on `to*`/default-1 on `from*`. Validated against reference vectors (red/green/blue/white OKLab incl. **blue `a=-0.032457`** corrected from the first draft, red OKLCH `h=29.234°`, mid-gray luminance 0.21404, white/black contrast 21, deltaE white/black 1). **Zero `apps/**`** — pure engine, no PT assumptions (see memory [[engine-reusable]]). `api-specs.md` `Color` row extended. Decision backed by a deep web-research pass (RSXform per-quad rotation is the standard Skia `drawAtlas` approach, **zero extra draw calls** — rotation is a vertex attribute; a single RSXform is a similarity transform so **non-uniform-scale-while-rotating and one-axis mirrors are inexpressible**; a 180° flip is a free vertical+horizontal mirror; pre-baked/angle-bucket frames are for discrete-angle retro pipelines, not continuous aim). **Engine half** (`packages/render`+`packages/react`): new optional `SpriteData.rotation` (radians, CW, default 0, about the sprite **anchor**); `SpriteDrawCommand` gains `rotation`/`anchorX`/`anchorY`; `draw()`/`drawRaw()` get a trailing optional `rotation = 0` (every existing call site unchanged); `fillXforms` computes the full RSXform `set(scale·cosθ, scale·sinθ, tx, ty)` with a **fast path** when `rotation===0` that is byte-identical to the prior code (proven to algebraically collapse to today's `tx=dstX`); zero-alloc. `api-specs.md` documents the field + the similarity-transform limit. Retires KG4's vertically-symmetric-frame workaround; unblocks future enemy-bullet/ship rotation. **Game half** (`apps/pan-tvardowski`): new pure `fx/exhaust-frame.ts` (`exhaustFrameIndex` sawtooth + `exhaustPulseAlpha` sine, spec-driven `EXHAUST_HZ`/min/max, zero-alloc); `systems/exhaust.ts` `updateExhaust` (pre-created `exhaustQuery`, module-scope `FIRE_FRAME_NAMES[20]`, `EXHAUST_OFFSET_Y`, role rotation consts); `Exhaust` component; `spawnExhaustFlame` called **internally** by `spawnPlayer`/`spawnEnemy`/`spawnBoss`/`spawnMiniboss` (owner-chosen auto-spawn — covers deep boss-FSM/split sites with zero per-site wiring); orphan-flame reap in `cleanup.ts`; `play.ts` integration (`exhaustFlickerAccum`, `exhaustQuery`, gate on `joystickState.active` — wired in play.ts not the row-suggested `player.ts`). **Orientation:** Kenney `fire00` plume points up → enemy flame above hull `rotation=0`, player flame below hull `rotation=Math.PI`; enemy always-on, player hidden at rest, reducedMotion→pinned max opacity. **Image/frame coupling is safe** (both set from the kenney sheet together; shape-atlas placeholder + alpha 0 until kenney decodes). Game integration & zero-alloc lenses passed; test-quality gaps → added `image===kenney.image` coupling assert, orphan-cleanup, boss/miniboss, multi-enemy-no-crosstalk, gate-transition, `kenney.image=null` tests + dropped `toBeDefined`.
