- **KENG1 — Engine: PNG bytes → SkImage decode in `@flare-engine/assets`** (2026-06-03, session #156). New `packages/assets/src/decode-image.ts`: `decodeImage(Skia, bytes): SkImageLike | null` → `Skia.Image.MakeImageFromEncoded(Skia.Data.fromBytes(bytes))` via a narrow injected `SkiaImageDecodeModuleLike` (local opaque `SkDataLike` handle), mirroring `procedural-atlas.ts`'s `SkiaAtlasModuleLike` injection so `assets` imports no Skia at runtime. No throw — returns `null` on decode failure (matches the real Skia contract; caller owns fallback). Barrel exports the fn + both types; one api-specs row. 4 behavioral tests (assets 29→33): success path returns the `SkImageLike` with readable `width()/height()`, null path, `fromBytes` receives the exact input bytes, and the `SkData` sentinel from `fromBytes` is the one passed to `MakeImageFromEncoded` (the two-call composition, not just compilation). **Gate/IDE gotcha:** the two call-capture tests first used `let x: T | null = null` mutated inside the mock closure — TS narrows that to `null` at the `expect()` site (picks bun's `(expected: null)` overload, so `.toBe(realValue)` fails). Only the root tsconfig (`include: packages/*/tests`) surfaced it in-IDE; the per-package `typecheck` gate is `tsc --noEmit` over `src` only, so it never sees test files. Fixed by capturing into typed call-arrays + `toHaveLength(1)`. The only hard bitmap blocker for Arc K — unblocks KG2 (the `texture-packer`/`image` loader). See memory [[typecheck-gate-skips-tests]].

- **Rain realism — full 2D falling-streak rewrite (engine + game)** (2026-06-02, session #155). The #154 first pass (hashed-streak + color uniform) shipped but on A54 read as flashing vertical "god-ray walls" washing out the scene — root cause was a wrong MODEL, not tuning: the lifecycle `mod(weatherScrollY*speedMul + phase*300, streakLen)` depended only on column+time, never the fall-axis pixel, so whole columns blinked in unison; streaks were 30–70% of column width (no thin drops, no dropout) and additively blended (washout). Deep audit (2 web-research passes — first surfaced 3D-perspective/lens refs the owner correctly rejected as off-domain; second found the 2D-fit Godot "Simple rain/snow" CC0 shader) reframed rain for a 2D top-down vertical-scroller as screen-space discrete falling streaks. **Engine** (`packages/postfx`): replaced both the `COMPOSITE_SKSL` rain branch and standalone `WEATHER_RAIN_SKSL` with a loop-free column-sawtooth model — rotated UV (wind tilt free), per-column hash dropout (`weatherDensity`), `fract(fallAxis*tiling − scroll + phase)` so streaks fall+recycle, head→tail `smoothstep`, thin X-mask, 2 parallax layers `max`-combined (near COLS45/tiling6, far COLS85/tiling11), alpha-over `mix` blend (no washout), refraction tap dropped. `weatherScrollY`/`scrollOffsetY` are now normalized [0,1) phases (was raw px). +2 uniforms `weatherDensity`(0.5)/`weatherStreakLen`(0.15): composite 22→24 floats, standalone 8→10. postfx 134→155. **Game** (`apps/pan-tvardowski`): scroll accumulator wraps to [0,1) (`SCROLL_K=1/40`, kills precision flicker); `ZoneFxDef` gained `density?`/`streakLen?` optionals + `setZoneFxDef` passthrough; Z1 drizzle (density 0.45/streakLen 0.12) + Z3 downpour (density 0.7/streakLen 0.2); SCHEMA_CANARY → c17818fb. PT 1201→1212. **Decision:** fullscreen SkSL shader over ECS particles (1 draw call at any density vs thousands of drop entities into the mid-tier-mobile entity wall). **Owner-verified on A54: "rain looks very nice."** Look is retunable from `zone-fx.ts` alone (density/streakLen/speed/weatherColor + a canary rehash), no shader edit. Closes Arc R (supersedes the #154 hashed-streak approach). Two gate fixes during close: runtime line-wrap (#154), `0.90→0.9` trailing-zero ×3 (#155). See memory [[rn-skia-postfx-pattern]], [[visual-spec-optional-field-typecheck]].

- **R1 — Engine: hashed-streak rain rewrite + configurable rain color** (2026-06-02, session #154). Rewrote the `COMPOSITE_SKSL` rain branch (and re-synced the standalone `WEATHER_RAIN_SKSL`, kept because it backs the public `createWeatherRain` effect) from the old `fract(u*0.04)` periodic comb — the cause of symmetric full-height lines — to: hash-bucketed columns (`col=floor(u2*0.04)`, 5 hashes → X-jitter / streak-width / brightness / fall-speed / phase), a per-streak Y lifecycle via `mod(scrolled + phase*300.0, streakLen)` with head+tail fade, two octaves (near freq 0.04/len 150/weight 0.7, far 0.07/len 90/0.3) for depth, and a refraction `src.eval(coord + offset)` tap. **Owner-approved scope change:** the baked blue tint became a configurable `weatherColor` vec3 uniform (generic name — other weather branches can tint off it later) appended at composite indices [19–21] (19→**22 floats**), default `[1,1,1]` so behavior is unchanged until the game overrides; parity `color` uniform on the standalone (5→**8 floats**, [5–7], default white). `scaleIntensity` untouched (color isn't intensity); other 3 weather branches byte-for-byte unchanged. Files: `packages/postfx/src/{shaders,effect,runtime}.ts` + `tests/{composite,weather-effects}.test.ts` + `docs/design/api-specs.md`. Tests **121→134 (+13)**: layout count bumps, default-color pins, a regression guard that the rain branch reads `weatherColor` and no longer contains the baked `rain*0.5/0.55/0.65` tint, plus algorithm pins (hash bucketing, `mod` lifecycle, far-octave `0.07`, refraction tap) on both shaders. Lint/typecheck green (one Biome line-wrap auto-fix in `runtime.ts` at the gate), build 39/39. **Game follow-up is R2** (wire `weatherColor` from `ZoneFxDef` visual-spec + A54 visual confirm — device read not possible engine-side).

- **High-DPI: physical-resolution scene texture for postfx** (2026-05-31, session #153). Closed the postfx-tail supersample row — but NOT via the row's `<Group scale>` idiom, which investigation proved cannot sharpen the scene (it only changes shader-eval resolution, not the source texel count). Root cause of the high-DPI softness: the scene SkPicture was rasterized at LOGICAL resolution (`usePictureAsTexture` called with logical size; picture recorded in logical coords) then bilinear-upscaled to physical by the Canvas CTM. Fix (engine, `packages/react` only, 2 files): `beginFrame` gains an optional `pixelDensity` arg → records at physical bounds with `current.scale(pd,pd)` baked in (only when pd≠1); `game-view.tsx` passes physical size to `usePictureAsTexture` + `pd` to `beginFrame`, displays `<Image>` at logical size — so the RuntimeShader image-filter `coord` stays logical and `resolution`/SkSL/`buildPostFxPasses` are UNCHANGED (confirmed from Skia `SkImageFilter` local-space semantics; a prior planning round proposed ×pd on `resolution` and was rejected as a regression since the working baseline already renders correctly with logical resolution). The native `<Picture>` fallback was DROPPED (a pd-baked picture double-scales through the native renderer's own `scale(pd)`); the no-postfx path now always renders via `<Image>` with zero RuntimeShader children. Perf: postfx ~0.2 ms supersampled on A54, well under budget; texture ~8–10 MB. react 97→104 (+7 behavioral tests). Build 39/39. **Owner-verified on A54 (2026-06-01): sharpness + effect centering confirmed.**

- **Shadow-rim deep-fix → CUT the dead lighting stack** (2026-05-31, session #152). Investigation killed the row's premise: RN-Skia `<RuntimeShader>` is an **image filter** (its single `uniform shader src` is bound implicitly by the image-filter pipeline — that's why composite chaining works), so it **cannot bind a 2nd texture child** (`shadowTex`) — the planned "fold shadow-rim in as a 2nd `<RuntimeShader>` with the shadow texture as a child" is impossible (`declareRuntimeShaderImageFilter` calls `MakeRuntimeShader(rtb, null, null)`). The only multi-texture JSX path is `<Shader>`+`<ImageShader>` (uses `makeShaderWithChildren`), which can't receive composite's image-filter output without reintroducing offscreen/readback. Separately, shadow-rim (§2b) had **never rendered on device** (the `apply()` path no-ops: nonexistent `MakeSurface` guard → `supported` always false, called with `source=null` → bridge early-return, Picture-replay drops SkSL drawRects), and §1 rim-light was orphaned dead code (`createRimLightRuntime` zero call sites, superseded by §2b). Owner chose to cut, not revive. Removed: PT lighting dir (shadow-rim + rim-light runtimes/SkSL + shadow-map + types + barrel), `visual-spec/lighting.ts` + `RimLightDef` + `RIM_LIGHT_DEFS` (SCHEMA_CANARY rehashed), Smok boss rim wiring (the rim-only `onPhase{1,2}Update`/`onPhase2Exit` hooks + `onRimLight*` `BossDeps`), `postfxRimLight` budget field; engine legacy fallback in the bridge (`applyPostFx` + `captureSceneSnapshot`/`getPostfxResult`/`clearPostfxResult` + `PostFxRuntimeLike.apply` + `renderPassToCanvas` + ping-pong/offscreen scratch + saved-viewport-dims). Live JSX postfx path (`setPostFxBinding`→`buildPostFxPasses`→`<Image><RuntimeShader/></Image>`, `renderShapesToImage`, `usePictureAsTexture`) untouched. PT 1265→1193, react 98→97; lint/typecheck/test green, build 39/39 FULL TURBO. **Revival path if ever wanted:** shadow-rim as a final `<RuntimeShader>` in the chain with the 256-bucket shadow map delivered as a **uniform array** (lookup only runs inside the boss disc — perf-trivial); needs array-uniform support added to `buildPostFxPasses`. See memory [[rn-skia-postfx-pattern]].

- **B5 + postfx pipeline rewrite (JSX RuntimeShader)** (2026-05-30). Closed B5 (PT-side wire of `ZONE_FX_DEFS` per zone via `setZoneFxDef`/composite controller) AND a major architecture rewrite of the entire postfx stack after manual device test revealed postfx had **never rendered on device** since the bridge was first written. Debug arc uncovered 7 layered bugs through 7+ engine-builder dispatches plus 2 multi-agent workflows. **Bugs found and fixed in order:** (1) `Skia.MakeSurface` doesn't exist on RN-Skia — real API is `Skia.Surface.Make`/`Skia.Surface.MakeOffscreen` (the bridge had been silently failing on `Surface === undefined` since postfx was wired); (2) bridge called `pass.shader.makeShader(uniforms, [source, ...children])` but RN-Skia requires `makeShaderWithChildren(uniforms: number[], children: SkShader[])` — children must be SkShader (wrapped via `image.makeShaderOptions(TileMode.Decal, TileMode.Decal, FilterMode.Linear, MipmapMode.None)`), not raw SkImage, and uniforms must be `number[]` not `Float32Array`; `applyRect` had to become a real SkRect via `Skia.XYWHRect(...)` not a plain JS object; (3) `Surface.Make` is CPU-backed (1fps slideshow due to full-screen GPU↔CPU readback per pass) — switched to `Surface.MakeOffscreen` for GPU; (4) GPU surface snapshots are uninitialized until `surface.flush()` is called before `makeImageSnapshot()`; (5) `Surface.MakeOffscreen` creates a separate `GrDirectContext` per call — texture-backed SkImages from one offscreen surface cannot be sampled on the main `<Canvas>`'s context (silently renders empty/black, confirmed via Skia C++ docs + RN-Skia issues #1052/#1513/#1811/Discussion #1359); fix: call `image.makeNonTextureImage()` on every snapshot before crossing contexts; (6) `<Picture>` replay drops `drawRect` commands whose paint carries a SkSL runtime-effect shader (proven by a hardcoded-red diagnostic shader that never rendered despite reaching drawRect with shaderNonNull=true) — RN-Skia limitation, not a logic bug; (7) the per-frame double `makeNonTextureImage` readback (scene snapshot + composite output) capped framerate at 10-30fps. **Architecture rewrites along the way:** (a) moved rendering logic out of `@flare-engine/postfx`'s `apply`/`applyPass` into the bridge so RN-Skia's full API surface (TileMode, FilterMode, MipmapMode, XYWHRect) was accessible; (b) collapsed the 4-pass ping-pong chain (vignette+bloom+grayscale-flash+weather) into a single `COMPOSITE_SKSL` shader with all effects gated by their own intensity uniforms — eliminates the multi-pass ping-pong entirely (`packages/postfx/src/shaders.ts` `COMPOSITE_SKSL`, 19 packed floats, branches on `weatherKind` 0..4); (c) FINAL architecture: postfx runs in the on-screen `<Canvas>`'s `GrDirectContext` via RN-Skia JSX components — bridge exposes `setPostFxBinding(runtime, chain)` + `buildPostFxPasses(...): PostFxPassDescriptor[]`, GameView calls `usePictureAsTexture(picture, sceneSize)` to convert the sprite SkPicture into an on-context texture, then renders `<Image image={sceneTexture}>{passes.map((p) => <RuntimeShader key={p.id} source={p.source} uniforms={p.uniforms}/>)}</Image>` — zero GPU↔CPU readbacks, zero cross-context handoffs, RN-Skia owns all compositing. **Also shipped this session:** ambience particle system removed (`fx/ambience.ts` + 4 test files deleted; user-confirmed redundant placeholder superseded by weather postfx); postfx budget bump 3→4 (silenced `frame-budget over limit` spam from the composite era); strict-SkSL hygiene sweep (`float2(1,0)` → `float2(1.0, 0.0)` etc. + `half3()` wrapper cleanup + uninitialized-local fix on COMPOSITE_SKSL + WEATHER_CLOUDS_SKSL + CRT_SKSL); diagnostic logging in `runtime.compile()` for null-return + uniform-float-count + `renderPassToCanvas reached` + `applyPostFx skipped: <reason>` (one-time-per-kind dedup — would have caught silent compile failures in 5 minutes instead of multiple cycles). **Composite shader (19 uniforms, fully restored from diagnostic):** chroma → CRT (warped src.eval inside out-of-bounds early-return) → bloom → weather dispatch (rain ranges <1.5 / sun-shaft <2.5 / dark-vignette <3.5 / clouds else; cloud uses hash+vnoise+fbm noise helpers) → grayscale flash → vignette → opaque alpha=1 return. **shadow-rim-runtime.ts:** typecheck-only fix (local `SkiaRuntimeEffectLike` interface renamed `makeShader` → `makeShaderWithChildren` + `Array.from(uniforms)` cast at the single call site) — shadow-rim's deeper API mismatches (Float32Array→number[], plain rect→SkRect) still unfixed and only fire during Smok-Krzemionkowski boss combat; flagged as separate follow-up. **Tests:** PT 1265 pass (from 1281 baseline after deletions for ambience-removal + obsolete capture/applyPostFx tests + 14 new zone-fx-wire tests + 11 new buildPostFxPasses tests + 5 new multi-pass-descriptors tests); postfx 121 pass (+19 composite + diagnostic logging tests, -19 deleted runtime-apply tests); react 98 pass (+ new buildPostFxPasses + game-view setPostFxBinding + multi-pass-descriptors; - applyPostFx/captureSceneSnapshot/getPostfxResult tests deleted as the API is now legacy-only). Build green (39/39, full turbo cache hit on un-modified packages). **User confirmation chain on device (A54 Galaxy):** initially black, then "all postfx now visible" with the cross-context fix, then 10-30fps with the readback approach — Path B JSX RuntimeShader refactor shipped to eliminate readbacks; user to retest framerate. **B5 + this entire arc closes the Presentation polish BG/FX track** (B1→B3→B4→B5; B2 shipped out-of-order session 2026-05-24). **Followups tracked as active rows for next session:** (i) shadow-rim-runtime.ts deep-fix (same API bugs the composite had — Float32Array/SkRect/etc.); (ii) supersample wrapper if high-DPI sampling reads blurry on 3x devices. **Not tracked in todo/plan (iOS-deferred per memory `device_smoke_devices`):** RN-Skia issue #3390 stress test on iPhone — Android A54 unaffected; queued for when iOS testing resumes. See memory `rn_skia_postfx_pattern` for the #3390 details. **Workflow tool used twice this session** — once to audit the empty-snapshot symptom (5 parallel research agents → cross-GrDirectContext finding cited Skia docs + RN-Skia issues), once to design the JSX refactor (4 parallel agents on RuntimeShader/usePictureAsTexture/ImageShader/real-world-patterns → grounded design + clean implementation). Ultracode mode was on for the latter half; investigations were definitive, fix grounded in cited evidence.

- **B4 — Engine: 4 weather postfx (rain/sun-shaft/dark-vignette/clouds) as postfx-bridge passes** (2026-05-26, session #150). Added 4 weather effects to `@flare-engine/postfx` (not `@flare-engine/render` — postfx is where the existing vignette/bloom/chroma/crt/god-rays/color-grading-lut chain lives; B5 imports from `@flare-engine/postfx`): `createWeatherRain` / `createWeatherSunShaft` / `createWeatherDarkVignette` / `createWeatherClouds` factories + matching `WeatherRainUniforms` / `WeatherSunShaftUniforms` / `WeatherDarkVignetteUniforms` / `WeatherCloudsUniforms` interfaces + 4 new `EffectKind` values (`weather-rain` / `weather-sun-shaft` / `weather-dark-vignette` / `weather-clouds`) + 4 SkSL constants (`WEATHER_RAIN_SKSL` / `WEATHER_SUN_SHAFT_SKSL` / `WEATHER_DARK_VIGNETTE_SKSL` / `WEATHER_CLOUDS_SKSL`). **Architecture deviation from row spec (well-justified, approved):** row recommended ONE shader with `kind` uniform branching; agent chose FOUR separate shaders to match existing postfx precedent (each of vignette/grayscale-flash/chromatic-aberration/crt/bloom/god-rays/color-grading-lut is its own shader); weather kinds are mutually exclusive per zone so branching offers no benefit, only complexity. **All 4 shaders use `uniform shader src`** (correctly bound by runtime as `children[0]` — different from B2's BG shaders where `src` was declared but never bound; postfx layers OVER the rendered scene, BG is solid-fill). **CRITICAL: no `packages/react/src/skia-bridge.ts` changes needed** — postfx dispatches via `SKSL_SOURCE: Record<EffectKind, string>` which TypeScript exhaustiveness-checks at compile time; extending `EffectKind` forces every key to be present, no silent fallthrough (this is the architectural distinction from the sprite pipeline's `resolveEffectPaint` that bit B2/B3 with the brown-2/3-block bug). Bridge wiring was the silent-failure point we'd been worried about — confirmed not applicable here. **Intentional schema asymmetry** between TS interfaces (wider, hold `direction`/`speed` for B5 passthrough convenience from `ZoneFxDef`) and packed Float32Array uniforms (narrower, only what SkSL declares): rain packs `[resW,resH,intensity,direction,scrollOffsetY]` (5), sun-shaft packs `[resW,resH,intensity,direction]` (4), dark-vignette packs `[resW,resH,intensity]` (3 — direction/speed unused per B1 schema JSDoc), clouds packs `[resW,resH,intensity,scrollOffsetX]` (4 — direction stored on TS for B5 angle decomposition into scrollOffsetX += speed*dt*cos(rad), not declared in shader); intent documented as one-line comment in `packUniforms`. `scaleIntensity` cases for all 4 kinds (intensity at packed index 2). SkSL algorithm sketches: rain = rotated streak modular tiling + vertical fade scrolled by `scrollOffsetY`; sun-shaft = 8-band radial god-rays from off-screen source point tilted by `direction`; dark-vignette = radial darkness gradient from center (same family as existing `vignette` but always black); clouds = 2-octave FBM noise scrolled by `scrollOffsetX` with top-60%-fade. **B5 must drive scroll accumulators each tick** via `chain.setUniforms(id, { ...uniforms, scrollOffsetY: prev + speed*dt })` for rain and equivalent for clouds (`scrollOffsetX += speed*dt*cos(direction*PI/180)`). **Tests: 28 new** in `packages/postfx/tests/weather-effects.test.ts` across 6 describe blocks (factory defaults ×4, factory overrides ×4, SkSL uniform/entry-point pins ×4, packUniforms layout ×8 with Float32Array length + intensity-slot-value pins, EffectKind exhaustiveness + chain.add ×4, scaleIntensity via globalIntensity ×4) — all behavioral assertions, no shallow snapshots. **Minor API correction during impl:** plan said `chain.list()`; actual API is `chain.all()` — tests use the correct method, no code change. **postfx 87 → 115 (+28). react 91 → 91** (no mock updates needed — react tests that mock `@flare-engine/postfx` don't enumerate EffectKind values). Build green (engine scope: build runs). Unblocks B5 (PT-side per-zone wire + A54 manual verify + demo capture).

- **B3 — PT: collapse 8 zoneN-bg.ts into one data-driven createZoneBg(def)** (2026-05-26, session #148). Replaced 8 structurally-identical per-zone files (`zone1-bg.ts`..`zone8-bg.ts`, ~680 LOC total) with a single `apps/pan-tvardowski/src/bg/create-zone-bg.ts` (~145 LOC) exporting `createZoneBg(zoneId, deps): ZoneBgController { destroy, tick }`. Parses `'z1'..'z8'` → numeric `1..8`, looks up `ZONE_BG_DEFS[numId]`, resolves `THEME_SPEC.colors[primaryToken/secondaryToken]` via `Color.fromHex`, dispatches via exhaustive 6-kind switch (inline `assertNever`) to the B2-shipped engine factories with per-kind density param routing (`density` int for roofs/forest, `waveScale` float for river, `craterCount` int for moon, `stalactiteCount` int for cave, `ridgeScale` float for mountain). Spawns 2 entities at z=-100/-90 (atlas frame `'tile-fire'` for all — SkSL shaders override pixels so frame choice is visually no-op). `play.ts` lost 8 import lines, 8 PlayState fields, 8 null inits, 8 if-blocks each in `clearWorld`+`setupZone` (one-liner replacements); gained `s.zoneBg?.tick(dt)` in `onUpdate`. **2/3-block bug diagnosed and fixed** (the row-spec primary visual issue: existing BGs rendered as a 2/3-from-top band, not fullscreen): root cause was `play.ts` passing `s.bounds.height` to the BG factory, which (a) fell back to hardcoded 640 pre-`onLayout` because `ctx.camera.viewportHeight=0` initially and (b) always subtracted the safe-area bottom inset even post-layout — so on a 932px-canvas device, BG entities were 640px tall (fallback) while camera renders world 0..932 = "2/3 from top" band. Fix: `ZoneBgDeps.camera: ZoneBgCamera` live reference + `tick(dt)` reads `camera.viewportWidth/Height` per frame and resizes both BG entities + recenters (by frame 2 after `onLayout` fires, they expand to true full-canvas; auto-handles orientation changes for free). **Deviations from plan:** (1) added `camera` field to `ZoneBgDeps` (required for fullscreen fix — surfaced explicitly mid-implementation, not in original plan); (2) `tick()` uses `world.get(...)` + in-place field mutation (`spr.width = vw; spr.height = vh; spr.effect = buildEffect(...)`) — the engine has no `world.set` API; `SpriteData.width/height/effect` are mutable. Effect interfaces are `readonly` so `effect` field replacement allocates 2 new effect objects per BG frame — acceptable per plan (BG is not the hot path); (3) deleted 8 stale per-zone test files (`zone-1-bg.test.ts`..`zone-8-bg.test.ts`), not 1 as the plan estimated. **Tests: net −7** (24 deleted from 8 per-zone test files; 17 new added in `tests/zone-bg.test.ts` covering — 8 entity-shape pins per zone with explicit fullscreen-coverage assertion that catches the 2/3-block bug if it regresses; 6 kind-specific density-param routing pins; 2 scrollSpeed `tick(dt)` integration pins; 1 destroy-removes-both pin). PT 1281 → 1274. **Net LOC: ~−600** (plan target −737; gap due to comments documenting the camera-sync rationale in `create-zone-bg.ts`). **First time PT actually animates the BG** — current `fire`/`fog`/`water` placeholder shaders were time-invariant; `tick(dt)` now drives `scrollOffsetY` via `def.scrollSpeed * dt` accumulator. Visual will change from ugly colored blocks to procedural silhouettes (the whole point of the arc); B5 owns A54 manual visual verify. Build skipped (game scope). Biome auto-fixes during run: import sort in test file + long-line wrapping in `riverWave`/`moonCrater`/`caveInterior`/`mountainHills` calls; one unsafe fix (template literal → string literal) applied manually.

- **B1 — PT: ZONE_BG_DEFS + ZONE_FX_DEFS schema (SOT lock)** (2026-05-26, session #147). Added `ZoneBgKindId` (6-kind union: `roofs-city` / `forest-canopy` / `river-wave` / `moon-crater` / `cave-interior` / `mountain-hills` — matching B2-shipped engine factory names) + `ZoneFxKindId` (4-kind union: `rain` / `sun-shaft` / `dark-vignette` / `clouds`) + `ZoneBgDef { zoneId, kind, primaryToken, secondaryToken, scrollSpeed, density }` + `ZoneFxDef { zoneId, kind, intensity, direction, speed }` to [visual-spec/types.ts](apps/pan-tvardowski/src/visual-spec/types.ts) (additive). New `visual-spec/zone-bg.ts` with `ZONE_BG_DEFS` (8 entries: Z1 Dachy Krakowa→roofs-city, Z2 Puszcza→forest-canopy, Z3 Wisła→river-wave, Z4 Łęczyckie Błota→forest-canopy, Z5 Łysa Góra→mountain-hills, Z6 Katakumby→cave-interior, Z7 Karczma→roofs-city, Z8 Księżyc→moon-crater — all 6 kinds covered, two repeats). New `visual-spec/zone-fx.ts` with `ZONE_FX_DEFS` (8 entries — all 4 FX kinds covered; rain ×2, sun-shaft ×1, dark-vignette ×3, clouds ×2). `ZoneBgDef.density` documented as a single semantic knob that B3's dispatcher routes to the kind-specific engine param (`density` int for roofs/forest, `stalactiteCount` int for cave, `craterCount` int for moon, `waveScale` float for river, `ridgeScale` float for mountain — schema honesty over premature type discrimination). `ZoneFxDef.direction` documented as unused for `dark-vignette` (B4 will ignore it for that kind). No new theme tokens needed — all 16 palette references (`terracotta`/`rust`/`moss`/`leafGreen`/`vistulaBlue`/`foamBlue`/`bogGreen`/`waterTeal`/`crimsonDeep`/`inkBlack`/`katakumbyDark`/`goldDeep`/`pitchBrown`/`bone`/`lunarInk`) already in `ColorTokenId`. Index barrel re-exports `ZONE_BG_DEFS` + `ZONE_FX_DEFS`; the 4 new types flow through the existing `export * from './types'`. SCHEMA_CANARY rehashed to `92e5f5d4c74a4a83113679e06f03899d4e6de464ac5b2a90837a45fee1e75f0e` with bump comment. **No runtime change** — pure schema lock per the visual-spec-arc pattern; zero edits to `bg/`, `scenes/`, `fx/`, or any consumer. **Tests: 40 new across 2 files** (zone-bg-spec.test.ts 21 tests / 68 expects; zone-fx-spec.test.ts 19 tests / 58 expects) — completeness (8/8 zones, no duplicates, exact key set), kind exhaustiveness (one test per union member + a canonical-list guard), token resolution (violations-array pattern catches drift with pinpoint diagnostics, chosen over 16 one-liner expects), field-range pins (scrollSpeed/density > 0; intensity ∈ [0,1], direction ∈ [0,360), speed ≥ 0), and 8 per-zone exact-value pin tests each (catch any future drift in the 5-field authoring rows). Plan estimate was ~52; actual 40 because token-resolution consolidated from 16 expects-per-zone to one violations-array test — same coverage, better failure diagnostics. **Biome lint deviation caught at gate:** initial commit ordered value imports before type imports in both test files; auto-fixed (zero behavior change) before re-running verify. **Total PT: 1244 → 1281 (+37)**. Build skipped (game scope). Unblocks B3 (PT BG wire — engine factory dispatch); also unblocks B5 (PT FX wire) once B4 ships the weather postfx passes.

- **E8 — Baseline: capture pre-A baseline run** (2026-05-26, session #146). Full matrix captured via TestingBot Maestro on Galaxy A55 + Pixel 6 + Pixel 10 (iOS skipped — Apple Dev #137 pending); 5 scenarios × 2 metrics × 3 devices = **32 rows in [docs/perf/bench-results.ndjson](../perf/bench-results.ndjson)**. Frame p95 range **0.93–9.52 ms** — all cells safely under 16.67 ms budget. Postfx p95 ~**0.03 ms** across the board (essentially free). Heaviest cell: particle-storm Pixel 10 = 9.52 ms (7 ms headroom remains). **Arc fixes shipped during capture (15 commits, see SESSION_LOG #146 for the full list):** (a) workflow installs `testingbot` CLI + gates commit-back on step outcome; (b) `~/.testingbot` `key:secret` file write (CLI ignores env vars); (c) device-matrix swap (Pixel 6a/A54 not on TestingBot → A55/Pixel 6/Pixel 10); (d) BENCH_DONE sentinel respects safe-area insets so Android virtual-button bar doesn't hide the Maestro target; (e) perf-report exfil via Maestro `copyTextFrom` + `evalScript console.log` → `maestro.log` (release-Hermes `console.log` doesn't reach logcat); (f) jq-emitted schema-correct NDJSON, testingbot stdout → stderr; (g) **architectural fix:** scenarios refactored from REPLACING the game loop (synthetic 0.004 ms empty-bracket measurements) to MODIFYING setup state while `play.ts` brackets the real loop with the recorder — methodology validated against GameBench / Reassure / oblador react-native-performance before rebuild. **n=1 caveat explicit in data:** same Pixel 10 boot varied 1.76 → 2.05 ms across two runs (~15% spread); device-vs-device rankings inside noise floor — lead with headroom-vs-budget framing. Nightly cron will accumulate samples for rolling 7-day median; revisit Tier-A scope after ≥7 samples per cell (E8 closes the Tier-E arc).

- **E4 — PT: Maestro flows for perf scenarios** (2026-05-24). Added 5 .yml flows under `apps/pan-tvardowski/maestro/`: `perf-boot.yml`, `perf-boss-smok.yml`, `perf-boss-lucyper.yml`, `perf-particle-storm.yml`, `perf-zone-transitions.yml`. **Deviation from row spec:** replaced `waitForAnimationToEnd` (defaults to ~5s, too short for 30s scenarios) with `extendedWaitUntil { visible: { id: BENCH_DONE }, timeout: 60000 }`. Added a pre-openLink `extendedWaitUntil { visible: "Play", timeout: 60000 }` so the deep-link listener (wired inside `bootstrap()` in `App.tsx`) is registered AND boot's save+audio init has completed before the VIEW intent fires — without this, Maestro's `openLink` immediately after `launchApp` could reach RN's `onNewIntent` before any JS listener exists, silently dropping the URL. Locale assumption: `en` (cloud: `--device-locale en` per #107; local AVD default). **Latent bug also fixed:** `apps/pan-tvardowski/src/scenes/boot.ts:50` unconditionally called `ctx.scenes.go('menu')` at the end of its async chain. If a deep-link (zone OR bench) arrived via cold-start `getInitialURL()` and called `scenes.go('play')` while boot was still awaiting `initAudio()` (up to 10s), boot's IIFE would tear down the play scene with a menu transition. The existing comment at `boot.ts:14` falsely claimed zone deep-links bypass the menu — they didn't in cold-start scenarios. Guard added: `if (gameState.currentScene === 'boot') ctx.scenes.go('menu')`. By the microtask boundary when boot's IIFE finishes its 10s audio race + sync work, play's `onEnter` has long since run and set `currentScene = 'play'`. **False alarms investigated and ruled out:** (a) iOS scheme — `expo.scheme: "pantvardowski"` auto-generates `CFBundleURLSchemes` during prebuild; existing `infoPlist` override (status-bar keys only) doesn't conflict. (b) BENCH_DONE mount race — React state batching is bounded by `extendedWaitUntil` polling. (c) `'boot'` / `'env-boot'` ID consistency — both fall through `play.ts:680` else-branch to `createBootScenario`. 1 new test (`boot-to-menu.test.ts`: race-guard pin — simulates deep-link mid-boot and asserts `go('menu')` is NOT called). PT 1206 → 1207. Build skipped (game scope). Unblocks E5 (release deep-link profile) and E6 (`bench-cloud.sh` / `bench-local.sh` will reference `maestro/perf-*.yml` paths).

- **E3 — PT: scenario library (5 scenarios)** (2026-05-24). Added `apps/pan-tvardowski/src/perf/scenarios/{boot,boss-smok-full,boss-lucyper-z8,particle-storm,zone-transitions}.ts` + `types.ts` + `constants.ts` + `index.ts` barrel; each scenario is a `ScenarioFactory(deps) → { init, tick(dt,elapsed): boolean, dispose }` driven from `play.ts` `onUpdate` when bench-mode is active. Termination: each `tick` returns `true` once `elapsed >= SCENARIO_DURATION_SEC (30)`, at which point the driver calls `emitPerfReport(recorder.flush())` then `markBenchDone()`. **`boss-smok-full` / `boss-lucyper-z8` device-path fix (caught in review):** the headless-only `_spawnBossEntity` helper attaches no `Sprite`/atlas, so on device the boss disc wouldn't render and the Tier-A rim-light / Tier-B 5-postfx-pass gates would measure zero — fix added optional `ScenarioDeps.spawnBoss?: (pattern, x, y, hpMax) => number` injected by `play.ts` (`(p,x,y,hp) => spawnBoss(s!.spawnCtx, p, x, y, hp)`); scenarios prefer it on device, fall back to `_spawnBossEntity` in tests. Boss scenarios hold the boss alive by clamping `hp.current = max(BOSS_HP_CLAMP_MIN=1, hp.current)` each tick after `updateBoss`; `boss-lucyper-z8` force-drains HP at t=10s (→ phase2) and t=20s (→ phase3) to exercise the full postfx phase 3. `particle-storm` calls `triggerExplosion('burst')` until `lifetimeQuery.count() >= BUDGET_LIMITS.particles` (480), max 3 explosions per tick. `zone-transitions` despawns every entity carrying `Lifetime` + `clearBossFsms()` every `WIPE_INTERVAL_SEC=5` to trigger the GC-spike path. Zero-alloc compliance: `ScenarioDeps` pre-creates `bossQuery`/`enemyQuery`/`bulletQuery`/`lifetimeQuery` once in `play.ts`'s bench-mode branch — no `world.query()` calls inside any `tick`. 38 new tests (`perf-scenarios.test.ts`): registry coverage, per-scenario section bracketing + counter presence, boss HP clamp pin, lucyper phase-drive at t=10/20s, particle-storm cap saturation, zone-transitions cycle count, idempotent termination, dispose multi-call safety, `spawnBoss` injection spy per boss scenario + fallback regression. Biome auto-fix on close: 2× unused `dt` → `_dt` in `boot.ts`/`zone-transitions.ts` (scenarios that only need `elapsed`). PT 1168 → 1206. Build skipped (game scope). Unblocks E4 (Maestro flows target the 5 scenario ids).

- **E1 — Engine: `packages/debug/createPerfRecorder` + JSON emit** (2026-05-20). Added `packages/debug/src/perf-recorder.ts` (176 lines) — `createPerfRecorder({ label?, ringSize? })` returning `{ beginSection, endSection, recordFrame, flush, reset }`. Pre-allocated `Float32Array` ring per section (default 1800 frames = 30s @ 60fps); circular drop-oldest for the percentile window, while min/max/sum stay **lifetime** (deliberate asymmetry: percentiles characterize recent window, lifetime catches worst-ever spikes — pinned in test #4). Percentile set: p50/p90/p95/p99 (p95 = E7's regression gate metric). Nearest-rank floor interpolation (`Math.floor(p * count)`), no linear interp. `flush()` is read-only; `reset()` is the explicit clear (mirrors `createBootTrace.dump`/`reset` pattern). Added `packages/debug/src/perf-output.ts` (7 lines) — `PERF_BENCH_TAG = '[perf-bench] '` (trailing space) + `emitPerfReport(report)` single-line `JSON.stringify` to `console.log` (not warn/error — avoids tripping CI parsers; logcat + iOS unified log capture all levels). Zero-alloc invariant on `recordFrame`/`beginSection`/`endSection` after first-seen setup proven **structurally** (no `new`, no array literals, no string concat in hot path) rather than via `heapUsed` deltas which are GC-noisy on Bun/Node — documented in code comment. Public types: `PerfSectionStats`, `PerfCounterStats` (Perf-prefixed for namespace clarity), `PerfReport`, `PerfRecorderHandle`. Tests: 13 across 7 `describe` groups (percentile ordering invariant, section bucketing, independent section tracking, no-op endSection, counter min/max/sum/mean, recordFrame-without-counters increments frameCount, zero-alloc structural check, JSON round-trip, sentinel-tag prefix, tag-suffix JSON parseability, `PERF_BENCH_TAG` literal pin, reset correctness). debug 46 → 59. api-specs.md +2 bullets under `@flare-engine/debug`. Build 39/39 green. Sibling to existing `createFrameBudget` + `createBootTrace`. Unblocks E2–E8.
